TL;DR: 针对现有On-policy自我蒸馏方法在推理任务中泛化能力差的问题，提出反思式同策略自我蒸馏（ROSD）框架，通过反思引导和错误定位蒸馏，将参考解模仿转为针对性推理修正，在领域内和跨领域推理基准上均取得更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OPSD方法在领域内推理增益有限，且对领域外问题泛化能力差，原因在于：1）自我教师模型条件化于已验证解，导致模仿训练域参考轨迹而非针对错误修正；2）对完整响应进行蒸馏会覆盖有效推理前缀并强化过拟合。

Method: 提出ROSD框架，包含两个核心机制：1）自我反思器（self-reflector）提取修正性思路（corrective idea）并定位首个错误跨度（error span）；2）修正性思路引导自我教师模型产生针对性监督，错误跨度限制蒸馏仅作用于需要修正的区域，保留有效推理前缀。

Result: 在多个领域内和跨领域推理基准上，ROSD相比标准OPSD方法在领域内推理性能更强，跨领域泛化能力显著提升。

Conclusion: ROSD通过将参考解模仿转化为反思引导的针对性错误修正，有效改善了自我蒸馏训练的效果和泛化能力。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) improves the reasoning performance of large language models (LLMs) by providing dense token-level supervision for on-policy rollouts. However, existing OPSD methods often yield limited gains on in-domain reasoning and generalize poorly to out-of-domain problems. We identify two key causes: conditioning the self-teacher on a verified solution encourages imitation of training-domain reference trajectories rather than error-specific correction, and applying distillation to the full response can overwrite valid reasoning prefixes and reinforce overfitting.
  We propose Reflective On-policy Self-Distillation (ROSD), a framework that turns reference-solution imitation into targeted reasoning correction through reflection-guided, error-localized distillation. For each rollout, ROSD uses a self-reflector to extract a corrective idea and locate the first erroneous span. The corrective idea guides the self-teacher toward targeted supervision, while the localized error span restricts distillation to where correction is needed. This design corrects flawed reasoning while preserving valid prefixes. Experiments on multiple in-domain and out-of-domain reasoning benchmarks show that ROSD yields stronger in-domain reasoning performance overall and substantially better out-of-domain generalization than standard OPSD. Code is available at https://github.com/ZiqiZhao1/ROSD.

</details>


### [145] [The Missing Piece in Pre-trained Model Evaluation: Reward-Guided Decoding Unlocks Task-Oriented Behavior Without Parameter Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28020)
*Shaobo Wang,Guo Chen,Ziyue Wang,Zhengyang Tang,Qingyang Liu,Xingzhang Ren,Dayiheng Liu,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出能量基解码（EBD），一种无需训练、奖励引导的推理时方法，通过外部轻量奖励模型调整预训练LLM的解码分布，使其在不经过后训练的情况下产生更符合指令的输出，从而提供更公平的基座模型能力评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练基座模型仅优化于下一词预测，在标准解码下常无法遵循指令或产生格式良好的回答，导致基准测试性能混淆了模型能力与解码失败。现有方法如后训练成本高，纯解码方法效率低且鲁棒性差。

Method: EBD是一种训练自由、奖励引导的解码框架。在解码过程中引入外部轻量奖励模型，通过奖励倾斜的目标分布引导生成，兼顾预训练模型先验与高效用响应。

Result: 在5个模型和6个基准上优于基线，Qwen3-8B-Base在AlpacaEval2.0上从8.8提升至44.5，Mistral-7B Math500延迟相比先前解码方法降低18.9倍，且对奖励模型大小鲁棒。

Conclusion: EBD能够激活预训练基座模型的指令跟随行为，使其输出更接近后训练模型，为基座模型提供更公平的推理时评估方式，且无需额外训练。

Abstract: With the rapid progress of large language models (LLMs), reliably evaluating the capabilities of pre-trained LLMs has become increasingly important. The challenge is that base pre-trained models are optimized for next-token prediction and often fail to follow instructions or produce well-formed answers under standard prompting and direct decoding. As a result, benchmark performance can conflate model capability with decoding-induced failures to produce task-oriented outputs, while exposing such behavior often relies on costly post-training. Recent decodingonly approaches attempt to reshape output distributions, but such methods can be inefficient and brittle across open-ended tasks. To address these limitations, we propose Energy-Based Decoding (EBD), a training-free, reward-guided framework for activating task-oriented behaviors from frozen pre-trained LLMs across both open-ended and objective tasks. EBD augments decoding with an external lightweight reward model, steering generations toward high-utility responses while anchoring them to the pre-trained model prior through a reward-tilted target distribution. We show that EBD shifts base-model outputs toward more instructionfollowing behavior, increasing behavioral similarity to post-trained counterparts and enabling a fairer inference-time evaluation of accessible pre-trained-model behavior. Empirically, EBD outperforms baselines across five models and six benchmarks, improving Qwen3-8B-Base on AlpacaEval2.0 from 8.8 to 44.5, reducing Mistral-7B Math500 latency by 18.9x relative to prior decoding work, and remaining robust to reward-model size.

</details>


### [146] [Beyond pass@k: Redundancy-Aware RLVR for Multi-Sample Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28022)
*Le Bronnec Florian,Alexandre Verine,Rio Yokota,Benjamin Negrevergne*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了代码生成模型中基于验证器的强化学习（RLVR）对生成程序冗余性的影响，发现仅追求正确性的RLVR会导致样本集中在重复实现上，而引入基于JPlag相似度的反冗余奖励可以有效提升有限预算下的可执行性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代码生成评估常用Pass@k指标，但近期RLVR方法虽然提升了代码正确性，却忽略了生成样本之间的冗余问题，可能导致在有限采样预算下性能不佳。作者希望探索RLVR目标对生成程序多样性的影响，并提出缓解冗余的方法。

Method: 使用JPlag抄袭检测系统衡量代码实现级别的冗余度；对比仅关注正确性的RLVR与Pass@k感知目标在冗余度上的差异；提出在RLVR中引入基于JPlag相似度的直接反冗余奖励来抑制重复生成。

Result: 仅正确性驱动的RLVR会导致生成集中在重复实现上；Pass@k感知目标能保持较低冗余并提升大预算性能；引入JPlag反冗余奖励后，在3个模型和3个基准测试上，有限预算下的可执行性能持续提升，效果可媲美甚至超越专门的Pass@k感知目标。

Conclusion: 代码生成中样本冗余是影响有限预算性能的关键因素，通过直接惩罚实现级别的相似性可以有效改善RLVR的训练效果，为代码生成模型的优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: LLMs for code generation are commonly evaluated in repeated-sampling settings using Pass@k, where multiple candidate programs are executed against unit tests under a finite sampling budget. While recent verifier-based reinforcement learning (RLVR) methods improve executable correctness, how these objectives affect redundancy among sampled programs remains poorly understood. In this work, we study implementation-level redundancy in code generation using JPlag, a plagiarism-detection system for code. Across models and benchmarks, we show that correctness-only RLVR often concentrates generations around repeated implementations, whereas Pass@k-aware objectives maintain lower redundancy and improve larger-budget performance. Motivated by these observations, we augment RLVR with direct anti-redundancy rewards based on JPlag similarity. Across 3 models and 3 benchmarks, discouraging near-duplicate generations reliably improves finite-budget executable performance, often matching or outperforming specialized Pass@k-aware objectives.

</details>


### [147] [Extracting Small Translation Specialists from LLMs by Aggressively Pruning Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28042)
*Liu O. Martin,Lucas Bandarkar,Nanyun Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从混合专家（MoE）大语言模型中激进地剪枝与翻译无关的专家的方法，无需大量重新训练即可压缩超过90%的参数，同时保持翻译质量基本不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM作为通用模型参数量巨大，对于机器翻译这一特定任务存在严重过参数化，导致不必要的内存和计算开销。

Method: 利用MoE模型中专家的专业化和多语言能力的可分离性，识别并剪枝与翻译任务无关的专家；无需训练即可剪枝，结合短时监督微调（SFT）可进一步压缩。

Result: 无需重新训练可剪枝50%专家无显著下降，70%仅轻微损失；短时SFT可剪枝75%恢复基线性能，部分设置下剪枝近90%仍保持合理翻译质量。

Conclusion: 机器翻译任务仅需LLM的很小一部分能力，MoE块（包含超过90%参数）可实现大幅压缩，验证了专家剪枝策略的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Modern large language models (LLMs) achieve state-of-the-art machine translation performance, but they do so as broad generalists largely trained for many tasks and capabilities unrelated to translation. Thus, they are heavily overparameterized for this task, resulting in excessive memory and compute requirements. In this paper, we present a method for aggressively pruning experts from modern mixture-of-experts LLMs while incurring negligible degradation in translation quality. Our approach exploits expert specialization and the separability of multilingual capabilities in LLMs to identify experts irrelevant to translation. And because of the modular nature of MoEs, these can be easily pruned without any training. Without retraining, we are able to prune half of all experts with negligible degradation and 70% with only minor losses. With a very short SFT, we prune 75% of experts while recovering baseline performance, and in some settings remove nearly 90% while maintaining reasonable translation quality. Overall, our results show that translation requires only a fraction of the LLM, enabling substantial compression of the MoE blocks that contain over 90% of parameters.

</details>


### [148] [Knowledge Dependency Estimation for Reliable Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28047)
*Chaodong Tong,Qi Zhang,Nannan Sun,Lei Jiang,Yanbing Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对LLM问答中知识依赖估计问题，提出Knot方法，通过子集级反事实监督和潜在依赖因子覆盖建模，实现无需穷尽扰动即可评估黑盒QA模型对不同知识单元的敏感性，在多项基准上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的问答不仅需要判断答案是否正确，还需识别预测所依赖的知识来源。现实LLM问答中，知识可能来自上下文、检索、分解或中间推理，形成噪声和冗余的候选空间而非干净的黄金证据集。现有方法缺乏对细粒度知识依赖的估计能力，且无法建模知识单元间的冗余性、可替代性和互补性。

Method: 提出Knot（结构化秩感知知识依赖估计器）：(1) 从子集级反事实监督中学习；(2) 通过覆盖潜在依赖因子建模子集敏感性；(3) 推导秩感知单元分数以识别影响力候选。无需在测试时进行穷尽扰动。

Result: 在多选题和生成式QA基准上，Knot在子集敏感性预测上优于所有基线；生成的单元排名比可部署基线更忠实，且无需额外QA模型调用；用于风险筛查时，依赖分数有助于早期标记易错预测。

Conclusion: Knot能有效估计黑盒QA模型对知识单元的依赖关系，建模冗余性和可替代性，为实际风险筛查提供可靠依据。

Abstract: Reliable question answering requires identifying not only whether an answer is correct, but also which available knowledge the prediction depends on. In realistic LLM-based QA, this knowledge may come from context, retrieval, decomposition, or intermediate reasoning, forming a noisy and redundant candidate space rather than a clean gold evidence set. We study \emph{knowledge dependency estimation}: estimating the sensitivity of a fixed black-box QA model to different candidate knowledge units. The challenge is to obtain fine-grained dependency scores without exhaustive test-time perturbation while modeling redundancy, substitutability, and complementarity. We propose \textbf{Knot}, a structured rank-aware knowledge dependency estimator. Knot learns from subset-level counterfactual supervision, models subset sensitivity through coverage over latent dependency factors, and derives rank-aware unit scores to identify influential candidates. Across multiple-choice and generative QA benchmarks, Knot outperforms all compared baselines in subset-sensitivity prediction and produces more faithful unit rankings than deployable baselines without extra QA-model calls; when used for practical risk screening, its dependency scores help flag error-prone QA predictions early.

</details>


### [149] [Prompting Is All You Need: Multi-view Prompting Large Language Models for Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28058)
*Nils Constantin Hellwig,Niklas Donhauser,Jakob Fehle,Udo Kruschwitz,Christian Wolff*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM-MvP方法，通过多视角提示、模式约束解码、上下文无关文法和前缀批处理，使大语言模型在方面级情感分析中达到或超越微调模型的性能，同时大幅降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在ABSA任务中虽通过少样本提示取得进步，但与微调模型仍有性能差距，且LLM推理的计算成本过高，难以实际部署。

Method: LLM-MvP融合多视角提示（考虑多种元素排序）、模式约束解码、上下文无关文法及前缀批处理技术。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上，LLM-MvP的性能与微调模型相当或更优，同时显著降低了计算开销，缩小了少样本提示与微调模型之间的差距。

Conclusion: LLM-MvP为ABSA任务提供了一种高效且实用的解决方案，使少样本提示方法在实际部署中具有可行性。

Abstract: Recent work explored the capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) in Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis (ABSA) through few-shot prompting, requiring substantially fewer annotated examples while achieving notable improvements over zero-shot baselines. However, a performance gap remained compared to models fine-tuned on hundreds of examples, and the computational costs of LLM inference present practical barriers to deployment. We introduce LLM-based Multi-View Prompting (LLM-MvP), which adapts the multi-view principle of considering multiple element orderings to LLM prompting. By combining schema-constrained decoding with a context-free grammar and prefix batching, LLM-MvP achieves performance competitive or superior to fine-tuned approaches while substantially reducing computational overhead. Extensive experiments across five benchmark datasets demonstrate that LLM-MvP closes the gap between few-shot prompting and fine-tuned models, offering a practical and efficient solution for ABSA.

</details>


### [150] [Challenges in Explaining Pretrained Clinical Text Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28060)
*Kristian Miok,Matej Klemen,Blaz Škrlj,Marko Robnik Šikonja*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LIME和SHAP等后验解释方法在临床NLP任务中的三大局限性：过度关注非信息性标记、归因不稳定、对不连贯输入变体仍给出高置信度预测，强调需要开发临床有意义、基于语义且对语言噪声鲁棒的解释策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床NLP中神经模型预测的解释仍然是一个重大挑战，尤其是针对涉及长文本、非结构化医学文本的复杂任务。虽然LIME和SHAP等后验方法被广泛使用，但它们在临床叙事文本中常常表现不足。

Method: 通过在住院时长预测任务上进行针对性演示，识别了基于标记和扰动的解释技术的核心局限。

Result: 发现三大问题：过度关注非信息性标记、归因不稳定、对不连贯输入变体仍给出高置信度预测。

Conclusion: 现有解释方法不适合临床场景，需要开发临床有意义、语义基础扎实且对语言噪声鲁棒的解释策略。

Abstract: Explaining the predictions of neural models in clinical NLP remains a significant challenge, especially for complex tasks involving long, unstructured medical texts. While post-hoc methods like LIME and SHAP are widely used, they often fall short when applied to clinical narratives. In this paper, we identify core limitations of token-level and perturbation-based explanation techniques through targeted demonstra- tions on a hospital length-of-stay prediction task. Our findings reveal issues such as overemphasis on non-informative tokens, instability in at- tributions, and high-confidence predictions for incoherent input variants. These results underscore the need for explanation strategies that are clin- ically meaningful, semantically grounded, and robust to linguistic noise.

</details>


### [151] [ConvMemory: A Lightweight Learned Memory Reranker, a Negative Attribution Result, and a Research-Preview Conflict Editor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28062)
*Taiheng Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种3.6M参数的小型学习型重排序器ConvMemory，通过交叉编码器教师监督在融合稠密+词汇特征上训练，用于对话长期记忆检索。在LongMemEval上以12-47x更低延迟达到超过BGE-large交叉编码器的Recall@10，但作者诚实指出其核心机制是低成本交叉编码器蒸馏而非时序结构利用，并通过严格的消融实验证明学习到的时间窗口虽在聚合上显著但缺乏时序特异性。同时发布了CCGE-LA候选集编辑器研究预览。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话长期记忆检索方法在效率和效果之间难以平衡，大规模交叉编码器延迟高、成本昂贵，而小型模型往往效果不佳。作者希望构建一个极小参数量的高效重排序器，同时严谨审视先前声称的时序机制是否真正成立。

Method: 通过交叉编码器教师监督蒸馏，在融合的稠密（dense）和词汇（lexical）特征空间上训练ConvMemory。使用五种子重训练消融实验与配对bootstrap统计检验，严格评估学习到的时间窗口机制的有效性。还提出了低幅度冲突感知候选集编辑器CCGE-LA。

Result: 在LongMemEval上，ConvMemory以12-47x更低延迟超越BGE-large交叉编码器的Recall@10；在Clean500上距离mxbai-rerank-large-v1仅差0.025 Recall@10但快28x；在Stress1000干扰项下差距扩大到0.081但延迟低117x。消融实验显示时间窗口在聚合上显著但缺乏时序特异性，最大效果出现在硬非时序控制组，对多跳时序查询无显著效果。CCGE-LA在LoCoMo的supersession和stale/rescue切片上有适度但一致的提升。

Conclusion: ConvMemory的核心优势来自廉价的交叉编码器蒸馏到融合稠密+词汇特征空间，而非对时序结构的真正利用。作者以诚实透明的态度报告了方法局限性，效果未达到mxbai-rerank-large-v1的绝对水平，且为单作者、未经独立审计的初步工作。

Abstract: We describe ConvMemory, a small 3.6M-parameter learned reranker for conversational long-term memory retrieval, trained with cross-encoder teacher supervision over fused dense and lexical features. On the LongMemEval memory family, ConvMemory operates above the BGE-large cross-encoder in Recall@10 at 12-47x lower latency, remains within 0.025 Recall@10 of mxbai-rerank-large-v1 on Clean500 while running 28x cheaper; under Stress1000 distractors the Recall@10 gap widens to 0.081 but ConvMemory still operates at 117x lower latency; these LongMemEval numbers are single-run or single-seed and are reported as indicative cost-frontier evidence, not benchmark-grade. We then publish a rigorous negative attribution result on a previously claimed mechanism: a five-seed retrained ablation with paired bootstrap shows that ConvMemory's learned temporal window is statistically significant on aggregate but not temporally specific, with the largest effects on hard non-temporal controls and no significant effect on multi-hop temporal queries. The honest description of the mechanism is cheap cross-encoder distillation in a fused dense+lexical feature space, not temporal-structure exploitation. We additionally release CCGE-LA, a low-amplitude conflict-aware candidate-set editor over ConvMemory, as a research preview with modest but consistent gains on supersession and stale/rescue slices on LoCoMo. All results are retrieval-stage; ConvMemory does not match mxbai-rerank-large-v1 in absolute LoCoMo MRR, and the report is single-author and not yet independently audited.

</details>


### [152] [PromptEmbedder:: Efficient and Transferable Text Embedding via Dual-LLM Soft Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28066)
*Yu-Che Tsai,Kuan-Yu Chen,Yuan-Hao Chen,Yu-Han Chang,Ching-Yu Tsai,Yu-Hsiang Chuang,Shou-De Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PromptEmbedder双LLM框架，通过可微提示生成将嵌入知识与骨干权重解耦，在新架构上仅需重训练轻量线性对齐矩阵，在MTEB基准上达到与LoRA相当性能，同时GPU内存减少40%，训练加速3.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM文本嵌入方法（如LoRA）在面对新骨干架构时需要从头重训练，存在计算效率瓶颈和跨架构迁移性差的问题。

Method: 提出PromptEmbedder双LLM框架：一个Prompting LLM通过连续松弛的可微生成过程为冻结的Embedding LLM生成指令感知的软提示，确保对比训练中梯度完全流动；任务特定知识定位在Prompting LLM中，适配新架构仅需重训练线性对齐矩阵。

Result: 在MTEB基准上，PromptEmbedder性能与LoRA微调相当，GPU内存减少40%，训练速度提升3.7倍。

Conclusion: 建立了一种可扩展、架构无关的高效LLM表示学习范式。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable efficacy in text embedding, yet current adaptation methods like LoRA face significant bottlenecks in computational efficiency and cross-architecture transferability. Whenever a new backbone emerges, existing approaches require costly retraining from scratch. To address this, we propose PromptEmbedder, a novel dual-LLM framework that decouples embedding knowledge from specific backbone weights. PromptEmbedder utilizes a Prompting LLM to generate instruction-aware soft prompts for a frozen Embedding LLM via a differentiable generation process with continuous relaxation, ensuring full gradient flow during contrastive training. By localizing task-specific knowledge within the Prompting LLM, adapting to new architectures requires only retraining a lightweight linear alignment matrix. Evaluations on the MTEB benchmark show that PromptEmbedder achieves comparable performance with LoRA finetuning while reducing GPU memory by 40% and accelerating training by 3.7x. Our approach establishes a scalable, architecture-agnostic paradigm for efficient LLM-based representation learning.

</details>


### [153] [StoryLens: Preference-Aligned Story Rewriting via Context-Aware Narrative Enrichment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28073)
*Hanwen Cui,Yuting Mei,Yuhang Fu,Dingyi Yang,Qin Jin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出面向个性化故事改写的STORYLENSBENCH基准、STORYLENSEVAL奖励模型和STORYLENSWRITER两阶段改写模型，通过上下文感知的叙事丰富而非单纯风格迁移，实现读者偏好对齐，实验表明该方法显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统风格迁移仅做表层改编，对读者满意度提升有限（仅2.3%）；而上下文增强的改写能大幅提升偏好对齐（24.5%），因此需要更有效的个性化故事改写方法。

Method: 构建STORYLENSBENCH基准（含结构化故事书、多维度读者偏好画像及排序改写）；提出STORYLENSEVAL奖励模型评估满意度；设计STORYLENSWRITER两阶段模型（监督微调+GRPO强化学习）。

Result: STORYLENSWRITER在忠实度、连贯性和读者满意度上持续优于强生成与个性化基线。

Conclusion: 上下文感知的叙事丰富对于个性化故事改写至关重要，超越单纯风格迁移的局限。

Abstract: Story rewriting aims to adapt existing narratives to diverse reader preferences while preserving plot consistency and narrative coherence. Unlike conventional work on style transfer, we argue that effective story rewriting demands context-aware narrative enrichment beyond surface-level stylistic adaptation. Our pilot human study shows that style adaptation alone provides only marginal gains in reader satisfaction (2.3%), while context-enhanced rewriting substantially improves user preference alignment (24.5%). Motivated by this, we introduce STORYLENSBENCH, a large-scale benchmark for preference-aligned story rewriting, comprising structured story books, multi-dimensional reader preference profiles, and ranked context-aware rewritten stories. Building on this benchmark, we propose STORYLENSEVAL, a reward model for estimating reader satisfaction over rewritten stories, and STORYLENSWRITER, a two-stage rewriting model combining supervised fine-tuning with GRPO-based reinforcement learning. We further establish a comprehensive evaluation framework covering fidelity, coherence, and reader satisfaction. Experimental results demonstrate that STORYLENSWRITER consistently outperforms strong generation and personalization baselines, highlighting the importance of context-aware narrative enrichment for personalized story rewriting.

</details>


### [154] [ATLAS: All-round Testing of Long-context Abilities across Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28079)
*Deli Huang,Cunguang Wang,Hongyin Tang,Zhe Tang,Linsen Guo,Dongyu Ru,Ruoshi Yuan,Ziyue Zhu,Xiaoyu Li,Ziwen Wang,Chen Zhang,Anchun Gui,Wen Zan,Jiaqi Zhang,Xuezhi Cao,Jingang Wang,Xunliang Cai,Yixin Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ATLAS是一个重新定义长上下文评估的基准框架，提出分层分类法、长度感知AUC评分和ATLAScore聚合指标，在8个能力维度、9个可审计组件和6438个实例上评估了26个模型，发现模型排名随上下文长度和任务类别显著变化，7个模型在两个长度范围内排名变动至少2位，两个分类层仅共享61%的跨模型方差，表明长上下文质量应按能力和长度分别报告而非单一分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文语言模型评估通常只报告单一长度或狭窄任务族的结果，掩盖了两个失败模式：性能可能随长度增长而崩溃，且强检索能力不一定迁移到下游使用。因此需要一套更全面的评估框架来揭示这些隐藏问题。

Method: ATLAS框架包含三个方法论原则：(i)分层分类法，将基础操作与应用工作负载分离以便归因失败；(ii)长度感知AUC评分，在固定的8K-1M网格上整合分数-长度曲线，用完整退化曲线替代单点指标；(iii)ATLAScore，对分类法类别进行调和平均聚合，惩罚不平衡轮廓，并通过端到端不确定性传播从子集分数到非线性最终聚合。

Result: 在8个能力维度、9个可审计组件和6438个实例上评估了26个模型。Gemini-3.1-Pro-Preview在128K处领先，Claude-Opus-4.6在1M处领先。ATLASscore@8K-128K和ATLASscore@8K-1M之间的排名显著变化：7个模型移动至少2个位次，两个分类层仅共享61%的跨模型方差，单个排名差距可达12个位置。

Conclusion: 长上下文质量应按能力和长度分别报告，而非使用单一总分。现有评估方法掩盖了重要差异，ATLAS框架提供了更全面、细粒度的评估视角。

Abstract: Long-context language models now advertise context windows up to millions of tokens, yet evaluations typically report a single length or a narrow task family, masking two failure modes: performance can collapse as length grows, and strong retrieval need not transfer to downstream use. We present ATLAS, a benchmarking framework that redefines long-context evaluation as length-dependent capability profiling. ATLAS contributes three methodological principles:(i) a layered taxonomy separating foundational operations from application workloads so failures can be attributed, (ii) length-aware AUC scoring that integrates score-length curves over a fixed 8K-1M grid, replacing single-point metrics with full degradation profiles, and (iii) ATLAScore, a harmonic-mean aggregate over taxonomy categories that penalizes imbalanced profiles, with end-to-end uncertainty propagation from subset scores through the nonlinear final aggregate. We instantiate the framework across eight capability dimensions with nine auditable components and 6,438 instances, and evaluate 26 models. Gemini-3.1-Pro-Preview leads at 128K, Claude-Opus-4.6 leads at 1M. Rankings reshuffle substantially between ATLASscore@8K-128K and ATLASscore@8K-1M: 7 models move by at least two ranks, and the two taxonomy layers share only 61% of cross-model variance, with individual rank gaps up to 12 positions. These results support reporting long-context quality by capability and length, not by a single headline score.

</details>


### [155] [ConRAG: Consensus-Driven Multi-View Retrieval for Multi-Hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28093)
*Yikai Zhu,Kunfeng Chen,Qihuang Zhong,Juhua Liu,Bo Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ConRAG提出了一种共识驱动的多视角RAG框架，通过系统优化查询侧和语料侧，并融合关系、实体和文本信号进行多视角证据检索，在多跳问答任务上显著超越基线方法，最高比vanilla RAG提升26.9%，并使Gemma-4-31B在MuSiQue基准上达到新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG方法在处理复杂多跳QA时性能有限，主要存在两方面问题：一是仅关注查询侧的任务分解或语料侧的知识图谱构建，缺乏系统性优化；二是对多文档间的关系、实体和文本等多维证据利用不足，导致检索准确性不高。

Method: ConRAG框架的核心包括：(1) 系统优化查询侧和语料侧；(2) 利用多视角证据（关系信号、实体信号和文本信号）进行共识驱动的检索；(3) 融合多视图信息以提高复杂多跳QA的检索准确性。

Result: 在三个多跳QA基准上的实验表明，ConRAG在所有基线上均取得显著提升，平均性能比vanilla RAG提升最高达26.9%，且Gemma-4-31B在MuSiQue基准上取得了新的SOTA结果。

Conclusion: ConRAG通过共识驱动的多视角融合策略，有效解决了复杂多跳QA中检索不准确的问题，验证了同时优化查询侧和语料侧并融合多维证据的有效性，为RAG在复杂推理任务上的应用提供了新方向。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has emerged as a promising paradigm for enhancing large language models (LLMs) on multi-hop question answering (QA), which requires reasoning over evidence from multiple documents. Current multi-hop RAG methods generally focus on either query-side task decomposition or corpus-side knowledge graph construction. Despite their progress, these methods still struggle to achieve satisfactory performance on complex multi-hop QA tasks. To this end, we propose ConRAG, a consensus-driven multi-view RAG framework that effectively boosts LLMs on complex multi-hop QA. The core of ConRAG is to systematically optimize both the query and corpus sides and to leverage multi-view evidence (relation, entity, and text signals) for more accurate retrieval. Extensive experiments on three multi-hop QA benchmarks show that ConRAG consistently outperforms all baselines by a clear margin, e.g., up to +26.9% average performance gains over vanilla RAG, and enables Gemma-4-31B to achieve a new state-of-the-art record on the challenging MuSiQue benchmark.

</details>


### [156] [LegalGraphRAG: Multi-Agent Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Reliable Legal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28120)
*Zerui Chen,Qinggang Zhang,Zhishang Xiang,Zhimin Wei,Linfeng Gao,Xiao Huang,Zhihong Zhang,Jinsong Su*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LegalGraphRAG提出了一种分层法律图谱结合多智能体系统的框架，通过分层组织法律知识图谱并在检索后引入证据验证机制，显著提升了图检索增强生成在法律推理任务中的准确性和可解释性，达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GraphRAG方法在法律领域面临两大挑战：法律语料具有异质性（包含案例、法条、解释等多粒度知识），平铺知识图谱无法区分事实细节、适用规则和抽象原则，导致检索不准确；法律判决要求透明且基于证据的推理，但传统RAG直接将检索结果送入LLM而不验证，导致推理过程不透明且易出错。

Method: LegalGraphRAG包含两个核心组件：（1）分层法律图谱（Hierarchical Legal Graph），对法律来源进行层级化组织，实现不同抽象级别的检索；（2）多智能体系统，包含Researcher（检索候选证据）、Auditor（严格验证证据与源文档的一致性）和Adjudicator（综合验证后的证据做出最终判决）。

Result: 大量实验表明LegalGraphRAG在准确且可信的法律分析任务上达到了SOTA性能，显著优于现有GraphRAG基线方法。

Conclusion: LegalGraphRAG通过分层法律图谱和多智能体验证机制，有效解决了法律领域异质性知识检索和推理可靠性问题，为法律智能推理提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: Graph-based Retrieval-Augmented Generation (GraphRAG) advances flat document retrieval by structuring knowledge as relational graphs, enabling more coherent and effective reasoning. However, applying it to specific domains like legal reasoning faces critical challenges. (i) Legal corpora are heterogeneous, containing multi-granular knowledge from cases, articles and interpretations. A flat knowledge graph cannot adequately differentiate between factual details, applied rules, and abstract principles, limiting accurate retrieval. (ii) Reliable legal judgment demands transparent, evidence-based reasoning. Traditional RAG passes retrieved context directly to an LLM without verification, resulting in opaque, error-prone reasoning. To this end, we propose LegalGraphRAG, a framework designed for reliable legal reasoning. Our approach introduces two core components: a hierarchical legal graph that hierarchically organizes legal sources to enable retrieval at appropriate abstraction levels, and a multi-agent system for reliable legal reasoning, where a Researcher retrieves candidate evidence, an Auditor rigorously verifies its validity against source documents, and an Adjudicator synthesizes the set of verified evidence to render a final judgment. Extensive experiments show that LegalGraphRAG achieves the state-of-the-art performance, outperforming existing GraphRAG baselines in accurate and trustworthy legal analysis. Our code, datasets and implementation details are available at https://github.com/XMUDeepLIT/LegalGraphRAG.

</details>


### [157] [Risk-aware Selective Prompting for Hallucination Mitigation in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28123)
*Yuang Huang,Yafeng Zhang,Yu Zilan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 系统研究了大视觉语言模型中基于提示的验证方法，发现其效果依赖于输入难度：对困难输入有效，但对简单输入可能有害。这种行为源于注意力重新分配而非视觉接地改善。据此提出无需训练的风险感知选择性提示方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于提示的验证被广泛用于缓解LVLM的幻觉问题，但其何时有效、何时无效的机制尚不清楚，需要系统研究以理解其行为特征和局限性。

Method: 1) 系统实验：在两种代表性LVLM架构和两个幻觉基准上研究验证提示的效果；2) 注意力分析：比较验证提示与中性提示的注意力分布和熵模式；3) 提出RSP方法：利用生成前的不确定性信号选择性触发验证。

Result: 验证提示对困难输入有效修正，但对简单输入会引入新错误；其机制是促使注意力从视觉token向指令token重新分配，而非改善视觉接地；RSP方法能在保持基线性能的同时缓解持续验证带来的退化。

Conclusion: 验证提示是一种有风险的干预手段，其效果高度依赖输入难度。选择性触发验证（如RSP）是更优策略，且有效选择信号因架构而异。

Abstract: Prompt-based verification is widely used to mitigate hallucinations in large vision-language models (LVLMs), yet when it helps remains poorly understood. We systematically study verification prompting across two representative LVLM architectures and hallucination benchmarks, and find that it is a risk-bearing intervention: its corrections increase with input difficulty, while newly introduced errors persist across difficulty levels. As a result, always-on prompting helps on hard inputs but offers little benefit -- and can harm -- easier ones. Our analysis further shows that this behavior is associated with a conservative output shift. Verification prompts redistribute attention from visual tokens toward instruction tokens and induce a distinct middle-layer entropy pattern absent in a neutral-prompt control, suggesting instruction-conditioned attention redistribution rather than uniformly improved visual grounding. Motivated by this input-dependent risk, we propose Risk-aware Selective Prompting (RSP), a training-free approach that uses pre-generation uncertainty signals to trigger verification selectively. RSP mitigates the degradation of always-on prompting while preserving baseline performance, and reveals that effective selection signals vary across architectures.

</details>


### [158] [Chinese Word Boundary Recovery through Character Alignment Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28128)
*Lusha Wang,Yuchen Li,Su Yuan,Jungyeul Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将中文词边界恢复视为基于对齐的投影任务，通过字符级对齐将目标端词边界投影回源端，以解决非标准文本（如学习者错误）中的分词脆弱性问题。同时引入了两个评估资源，实验表明该方法能有效纠正过度分词错误，比直接分词更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中文分词在处理非标准文本（如语言学习者错误导致的字符级偏差）时表现脆弱，这些偏差会破坏下游标注和评估所依赖的词边界假设，亟需一种鲁棒的词边界恢复方法。

Method: 将词边界恢复建模为基于对齐的投影任务：给定噪声源句和更干净的目标句，先在字符级别对齐两个字符串，再将目标端的词边界投影回源端。两步走方法：对齐+投影。

Result: 直接分词在学习者输入中容易产生复合词碎片化错误，而所提出的两步投影方法通过利用校正后的目标恢复源端词跨度，纠正了多数过度分词错误。词边界恢复与普通分词是不同的任务。

Conclusion: 对齐投影为在噪声输入下稳定中文标注和评估提供了一种原则性机制，词边界恢复与直接分词有本质区别。

Abstract: Chinese word segmentation is especially fragile in non-standard text, where language learner errors and other character-level divergences disrupt the word boundaries assumed by downstream annotation and evaluation. This paper formulates Chinese word boundary recovery as an alignment-based projection task. Given a noisy source sentence and a cleaner target counterpart, we first align the two strings at the character level and then project target-side word boundaries back onto the source. Beyond the recovery method itself, we introduce two evaluation resources: a manually checked learner Chinese benchmark based on MuCGEC and a controlled synthetic benchmark derived from the Chinese Penn Treebank. Experiments show that direct segmentation remains vulnerable to compound fragmentation in learner input, whereas the proposed two step projection method corrects many over-segmentation errors by using the corrected target to recover source-side word spans. The results show that word boundary recovery is distinct from ordinary segmentation and that alignment projection provides a principled mechanism for stabilizing Chinese annotation and evaluation under noisy input.

</details>


### [159] [Better heads do not guarantee better binarized constituency parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28131)
*Zeyao Qi,Yige Chen,Eitan Klinger,Vivaan Wadhwa,Jungyeul Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了基于标点感知的树二值化方法在成分句法分析中的应用，发现虽然基于学习的中心词预测显著优于规则方法，但在消二值化后并未带来一致的解析性能提升。标点条件评估表明，基于学习的中心词在宏观平均标点敏感F1上表现不如基于规则的二值化，且跨树库迁移时存在不稳定性。结论是更好的中心词预测并不意味着更好的标点敏感成分句法分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究基于依存关系引导的中心词（dependency-induced headedness）是否能改善二元解析器的监督信号，重新审视标点感知的树二值化方法在成分句法分析中的作用。

Method: 对比基于学习的中心词预测与基于规则的中心词预测在树二值化中的表现，通过标点条件评估和跨树库迁移实验进行系统分析。

Result: 学习型中心词在内在中心词预测任务上显著优于规则型，但在消二值化后未带来一致的解析增益；在标点敏感F1上宏观平均表现不如规则二值化，跨树库迁移时存在不稳定性。

Conclusion: 语言学上合理的中心词并不一定是解析器最优的二值化控制信号，更好的中心词预测不能保证更好的标点敏感成分句法分析性能。

Abstract: We revisit punctuation-aware tree binarization for constituency parsing and ask whether dependency-induced headedness improves binary parser supervision. Although learned heads substantially outperform rule-based heads in intrinsic head prediction, they do not yield consistent parsing gains after debinarization. In particular, punctuation-conditioned evaluation shows that learned headedness underperforms rule-based binarization in macro-average punctuation-sensitive $F_1$, despite a small overall gain on CTB. Similar instability appears under cross-treebank transfer. These results suggest that \ycc{linguistically grounded} headedness is not necessarily parser-optimal when used as a binarization control signal. The paper presents a negative result: better head prediction does not imply better punctuation-sensitive constituency parsing.

</details>


### [160] [DEPART: DEcomposing PARiTy across Multilingual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28163)
*Manan Uppadhyay,Prashant Kodali,Pranjal Chitale,Reshma Ramaprasad,Himanshu Beniwal,Sunayana Sitaram*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段贝叶斯分层框架，将多语言大模型的性能差异分解为可解释成分：语言特征（语系、文字等）可解释79%-92%的语言身份方差，其中模型对英语的表征相似性是首要预测因子；NLU和推理任务呈现不同的方差分布——模型身份主导理解任务（66.7%），而benchmark×模型交互主导推理任务（46.3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有mLLM排行榜只报告各语言准确率，但不解释性能差异的成因，导致系统性偏差无法归因，也未能给实践者提供可操作的杠杆。

Method: 首先通过无分布假设的Friedman和Kruskal-Wallis检验确认语言差距是系统性的而非采样噪声；然后提出两阶段贝叶斯分层框架：第一阶段将语言身份方差分解到可观察语言特征（语系、文字类型、类型距离等），并纳入模型对英语的表征相似性；第二阶段将完整（模型×基准×语言）立方体分解，量化不同方差成分的贡献。

Result: 语言特征可解释理解任务语言方差R²=79%、推理任务R²=92%；模型对英语的表征相似性是主导预测因子。在完整方差分解中，NLU任务中模型身份占66.7%方差，推理任务中benchmark×模型交互占46.3%方差，两者方差分布本质不同。

Conclusion: 多语言评估应从被动的性能映射转变为可解释、可诊断的框架，为缩小语言差距提供具体的操作杠杆。

Abstract: Multilingual Large Language Models (mLLMs) leaderboards report per-language accuracy but rarely explain why disparities emerge, leaving systemic biases unattributed and offering practitioners no actionable levers. We first establish that these gaps are systematic rather than artifacts of sampling noise via distribution-free Friedman and Kruskal--Wallis tests, then introduce a two-step Bayesian hierarchical framework that decomposes multilingual performance variance into interpretable components. First, isolating the variance attributable to language identity, we show that observable language features (script, family, typological distance) explain $R^2_{\text{ling}} = 79\%$ of this variance on understanding tasks and $92\%$ on reasoning, with a model's internal representational similarity to English emerging as the dominant predictor across both task buckets. Second, decomposing the full (model$\times$benchmark$\times$language) cube, we find that NLU and reasoning have fundamentally divergent variance profiles: model identity dominates understanding ($66.7\%$ of variance), whereas the benchmark$\times$model interaction dominates reasoning ($46.3\%$). Together these results recast multilingual evaluation from passive performance mapping into an explainable, diagnostic framework with concrete levers for targeting the root drivers of language disparity.

</details>


### [161] [SuperValid: Capability-Aligned OOD Validation for Generalizable Downstream Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28179)
*Quanen Sun,Changxin Tian,Ke Shi,Cai Chen,Cunyin Peng,Jia Liu,Kunlong Chen,Zhiqiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SuperValid框架，通过合成能力级别的OOD验证数据来预测大语言模型的下游性能，解决了传统缩放定律在基准级别和IID验证损失上的泛化局限。在6个能力领域、17个基准上的实验表明，SuperValid损失与下游性能具有强且稳定的相关性，可用于模型选择、早停和缩放决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缩放定律在预测下游性能时面临两方面局限：基准级别性能引入场景特定噪声，IID验证损失在训练分布变化时无法追踪能力提升。因此需要更通用的下游缩放研究方法。

Method: 提出SuperValid框架，在能力级别研究下游缩放，通过蒸馏基准中的核心概念并扩展为多样化的知识密集型文本来合成OOD、能力对齐的验证数据。

Result: 在6个能力领域17个基准上的实验表明，SuperValid损失与不同架构、规模和训练数据分布模型的下游性能之间具有强且稳定的相关性。

Conclusion: SuperValid作为无需基准评估、训练期间可计算的无训练度量，能够有效指导模型选择、早停和缩放决策。

Abstract: Scaling laws guide large language model training by relating compute to cross-entropy loss, and recent work further extends them to predict downstream benchmark performance. However, prior approaches face generalization limitations from two aspects: focusing on benchmark-level performance introduces scenario-specific artifacts, while relying on IID validation loss fails to track capability improvements when training distributions vary. In this work, we argue that downstream scaling should be studied at the capability level, which captures shared skill factors across related tasks while abstracting away benchmark-specific noise. We propose SuperValid, a framework that synthesizes OOD (out-of-distribution), capability-aligned validation data by distilling core concepts from benchmarks within a capability domain and expanding them into diverse, knowledge-rich texts. Extensive experiments spanning 17 benchmarks grouped into 6 capability domains show that SuperValid loss exhibits strong and stable correlation with downstream performance across models of different architectures, scales, and training data distributions. As a training-free metric computable during training without benchmark evaluation, SuperValid enables effective model selection, early stopping, and scaling decisions.

</details>


### [162] [When Confidence Misleads: Suffix Anchoring and Anchor-Proximity Confidence Modulation for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28181)
*Jungwon Park,Jimyeong Kim,Jungmin Ko,Nojun Kwak,Wonjong Rhee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现扩散语言模型在完全非自回归解码中，EOT令牌的高置信度会导致生成不完整，而插入后缀锚点虽能缓解此问题，但会引发锚点附近的过度自信导致过早解码。为此提出后缀锚点置信度调制方法，通过插入短后缀锚点并根据解码进度调制锚点附近置信度，在保持响应完整性优势的同时减少过早解码，在多个基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练无关的解码策略通常使用模型置信度来选择解码位置，但该假设在完全非自回归解码中存在缺陷：EOT令牌可能获得高置信度导致生成不完整，而后缀锚点又会引起局部过度自信。本文旨在解决这些置信度误导问题。

Method: 提出后缀锚点置信度调制（Suffix-Anchored Confidence Modulation），一种无需训练的方法：插入短后缀锚点以鼓励响应完成，同时根据解码进度调制锚点附近位置的置信度，从而减少锚点相邻令牌的过早解码。

Result: 在文本推理、视觉-语言推理和代码生成基准测试中，该方法一致提升了基于置信度的完全非自回归解码性能，优于显式的EOT抑制策略，并保持了完全非自回归生成的并行解码优势。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了置信度在完全非自回归解码中的误导问题，提出的方法在保持后缀锚点好处的同时有效缓解了过早解码，为扩散语言模型的解码策略提供了改进方向。

Abstract: Diffusion language models decode text by iteratively denoising masked token sequences, making the choice of which positions to decode a central inference-time decision. Most training-free decoding strategies use model confidence for position selection, assuming that high-confidence positions are ready to be decoded. In this work, we revisit this assumption by studying when confidence misleads fully non-autoregressive (fully non-AR) decoding. EOT tokens can receive high confidence and cause incomplete generation; inserting a suffix anchor can mitigate this issue but introduces local overconfidence near the anchor, causing anchor-adjacent tokens to be decoded too early. To address these issues, we propose Suffix-Anchored Confidence Modulation, a simple training-free method that inserts a short suffix anchor to encourage response completion and modulates confidence near the anchor according to decoding progress. This preserves the response-completion benefit of suffix anchoring while reducing premature decoding of anchor-adjacent tokens. Across text-only reasoning, vision-language reasoning, and code-generation benchmarks, our method consistently improves confidence-based fully non-AR decoding, outperforms explicit EOT suppression, and preserves the parallel decoding advantage of fully non-AR generation.

</details>


### [163] [BenGER: Benchmarking LLM Systems on Subsumption-Based Legal Reasoning in German Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28183)
*Sebastian Nagl,Ann-Kristin Mayrhofer,Martin Heidebach,Aleyna Koçak,Anne Zettelmeier,Elly Breu,Angelina Greiner,Sofija Milijas,Matthias Grabmair*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了BenGER数据集——一个用于评估大语言模型在德国法律归摄推理上的基准。该数据集包含596个考试式自由文本法律案例任务和531个短篇教义推理任务。研究评估了12种当代LLM系统，并通过人类基线对比（包括无辅助和人机协同两种条件），验证了LLM-as-a-Judge框架的有效性。结果表明，用LLM评判员替代盲审人员不会降低与人类评分池的一致性，闭源旗舰模型领先排行榜，人机协同显著优于纯人类工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏针对德国法律归摄推理的标准化评估基准，尤其是能够比较LLM与人类在法律推理任务上表现的数据集和评估框架。

Method: 构建了包含596个法律案例任务和531个教义推理任务的BenGER数据集；引入基于评分标准的LLM-as-a-Judge评估框架，并通过多评审者盲审（三位盲审加一位创作者评审）进行交叉验证；在受控验证子集上对比了无辅助人类、人机协同与LLM系统的表现。

Result: LLM评判员替代盲审人员后与完整人类评分池的一致性（Calderon r=0.96）与移除该评审员后的一致性（r=0.96）相当；闭源旗舰系统在所有语料库上领先排行榜；人机协同显著优于纯人类工作。

Conclusion: BenGER数据集可有效评估法律推理能力；LLM-as-a-Judge框架可可靠替代盲审评审员；人机协同是提升法律推理任务表现的有效途径。

Abstract: We introduce the BenGER (Benchmark for German Law) dataset for evaluating LLM systems on subsumption-based legal reasoning in German law. The BenGER dataset consists of three components: 596 exam-style free-text legal case tasks across multiple levels of legal education and 531 short doctrinal reasoning tasks. We evaluate 12 contemporary LLM systems -- closed flagship, efficiency-oriented, and open-weight -- across automatic and judge-based metrics. On a controlled validation subset of timed human-written solutions under both unaided and human--AI co-creation conditions, we contextualise model performance against these human baselines. We introduce a rubric-aligned LLM-as-a-Judge framework cross-validated against a multi-rater human-grading protocol (three blind reviews plus one author-informed creator review per solution). Our results show that replacing a blind human reviewer with the LLM judge degrades agreement with the full human pool no more than removing that reviewer altogether (Calderon r=0.96 vs.~r=0.96, matched n=30), that closed-flagship systems lead the leaderboard across all corpora, and that human--AI co-creation substantially outperforms unaided human work.

</details>


### [164] [Framing Matters: Addressing Framing Sensitivity in Decision-Making through Behaviorally-Grounded Value Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28188)
*Seojin Hwang,Minju Kim,Junhyuk Choi,JeongHyun Park,Hwanhee Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM在事实等价但框架不同的输入下决策不稳定（翻转率28.6%），现有干预无效甚至加剧问题，提出Valign方法通过内部表征级操作有效缓解框架敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被部署在法律推理等高 stakes 决策场景中，但事实等价的不同框架输入会显著 destabilize 模型决策，现有研究缺乏对此问题的系统性基准和缓解方法。

Method: 构建Fragile基准，沿价值色彩叙述、时间切片、叙事生动性三个受控维度隔离事实保持的语义框架；提出Valign方法，通过锚定稳定价值先验、引导隐藏状态向价值一致方向、投影移除时间-生动性敏感方向来干预表征层。

Result: LLM平均决策翻转率28.6%；简单提示层和激活层干预无效甚至放大框架敏感性；Valign持续降低框架导致的决策翻转。

Conclusion: 缓解框架敏感性需要直接针对框架操作的内在表征路径，而非表层干预。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in high-stakes decision-making settings such as legal reasoning, where consistency under factually equivalent inputs is critical. However, we find that fact-preserved but differently framed inputs can significantly destabilize LLM decisions. To systematically investigate this problem, we introduce Fragile, a large-scale benchmark that isolates fact-preserving semantic framing across three controlled dimensions: value-tinted narration, temporal slice, and narrative vividness. Our experiments reveal a high susceptibility of LLMs to framing, with an average decision flip rate of 28.6%. We find that simple prior prompt-level and activation-level interventions not only fail to suppress framing sensitivity but actively amplify it. We therefore propose Valign, a representation-level method that explicitly targets these framing dimensions by anchoring decisions to a stable value prior, steering hidden states toward the model's value-consistent direction, and projecting out temporal-vividness-sensitive directions from the model's hidden states. Valign consistently reduces framing-induced decision flips, demonstrating that robust mitigation requires directly targeting the internal pathways in which framing operates.

</details>


### [165] [The Harder Text Embedding Benchmark (HTEB): Beyond One-dimensional Static Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28190)
*Manuel Frank,Haithem Afli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出HTEB（更难文本嵌入基准），一种动态评估框架，沿着词汇/风格、长度和语言三个可解释轴挑战嵌入模型的鲁棒性。评估16个模型在32个数据集42种语言上的表现，发现：模型在不同轴上具有部分解耦的鲁棒性特征；规模提升能提高绝对分数但不会缩小原始与变换评估之间的差距；英语数据集对变换更敏感。因此主张嵌入鲁棒性应是多维的、动态的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入基准（如MTEB）将鲁棒性视为静态标量属性，报告单一分数，无法揭示模型对不同类型变化的差异化响应。作者认为嵌入鲁棒性本质上是多维的，需要动态评估来暴露静态基准隐藏的失败模式。

Method: 提出HTEB框架，通过LLM在评估时对输入进行随机变换，沿三个可解释轴（词汇/风格、长度、语言）挑战鲁棒性。在32个数据集上评估16个开源嵌入模型，涵盖42种语言，并通过4,800个人工评分验证英语子样本的变换质量。

Result: (1) 模型在不同轴上展现出特定且部分解耦的鲁棒性剖面；(2) 跨三个模型族，规模提升提高绝对分数但未缩小原始与变换评估间的差距，其中缩放主要改善语言轴；(3) 英语数据集比多语言数据集对HTEB变换更敏感。

Conclusion: HTEB能够识别模型在部署相关轴上的优势与短板，挑战了当前嵌入基准的单分数范式，主张采用多维、动态的鲁棒性评估方法。

Abstract: Embedding benchmarks like MTEB report a single score per model, implicitly treating robustness as a static, scalar property. We argue that embedding robustness is multidimensional, since models respond differently to different types of variation, and requires dynamic evaluation to expose failures hidden by static benchmarks. We introduce the Harder Text Embedding Benchmark (HTEB), a dynamic evaluation framework that challenges model robustness along three practically interpretable axes (Lexical/Stylistic, Length and Language) by stochastically transforming inputs at evaluation time with an LLM. Evaluating 16 open-weight embedding models on 32 datasets covering 42 languages under transformations validated by 4,800 human ratings on an English subsample, we find three patterns: (1) Models exhibit specific, partly decoupled robustness profiles across axes. (2) Across three model families, scale increases absolute scores but does not close the gap between original and transformed evaluations. Here, scaling tends to improve specifically the Language axis. (3) English datasets are more sensitive to HTEB transformations than multilingual datasets. This demonstrates that HTEB identifies strengths and weaknesses of models along deployment-relevant axes, challenging current embedding benchmarks and arguing for multidimensional, dynamic robustness evaluation.

</details>


### [166] [Pruning and Distilling Mixture-of-Experts into Dense Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28207)
*Junhyuck Kim,Jihun Yun,Haechan Kim,Gyeongman Kim,Joonghyun Bae,Jaewoong Cho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出首个将训练好的MoE模型系统性地转换为标准稠密架构的框架，通过专家评分、选择、分组和拼接形成稠密FFN，再通过知识蒸馏精炼。在350种配置下评估发现评分方法最为关键，所提出的多样性感知评分优于现有方法。在参数量匹配控制下，MoE转稠密相比稠密剪枝在约4B token蒸馏后平均下游准确率提升6.3个百分点，训练速度加快1.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构虽成为主流，但需将所有专家参数加载到内存中，不利于内存受限的部署场景。现有压缩方法仅减少专家数量，输出仍是MoE模型，未解决根本的内存限制问题。

Method: 提出系统化MoE转稠密框架：(1) 专家评分（评估7种评分方法，含提出的多样性感知评分）；(2) 专家选择；(3) 专家分组（评估5种分组方法）；(4) 拼接为稠密FFN；(5) 通过知识蒸馏精炼（含2种幅度缩放方法）。在Qwen3-30B-A3B上测试350种配置，并在DeepSeek-V2-Lite和GPT-OSS-20B上验证。

Result: 评分方法是影响性能的最关键因素，提出的多样性感知评分一致优于现有方法。在参数量匹配控制下，MoE转稠密相比稠密到稠密剪枝，在约4B token蒸馏后平均下游准确率提升6.3个百分点，训练壁钟速度加快1.6倍。

Conclusion: MoE转稠密是一种有效的模型压缩范式，既能解决MoE内存限制问题，又能在同等参数量下优于传统稠密剪枝方法，且训练效率更高。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) is now the dominant architecture for frontier language models, yet it requires all expert parameters to be loaded in memory, making it less preferable for memory-constrained deployment. Existing compression methods reduce the number of experts but the output remains an MoE model with the same fundamental limitation. We present the first systematic framework for converting a trained MoE into a standard fully dense architecture: experts are scored, selected, and grouped, then concatenated into a dense FFN and refined by knowledge distillation from the MoE teacher. We evaluate 7 scoring, 5 grouping, and 2 magnitude scaling methods across a range of selected expert counts on Qwen3-30B-A3B, yielding 350 configurations. We find that the choice of scoring method is the most impactful, with our novel diversity-aware scoring consistently outperforming prior methods on Qwen3-30B-A3B, DeepSeek-V2-Lite, and GPT-OSS-20B. Under a controlled comparison at matched parameter count, MoE-to-dense outperforms dense-to-dense pruning by +6.3 pp in average downstream accuracy after ~4B-token distillation at 1.6x faster training wall-clock speed.

</details>


### [167] [When Helpful Context Leaks: Privacy Risks in Domain-Adapted ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28211)
*Maike Züfle,Jan Niehues*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文识别并系统研究了语音LLM领域定制化（提示词注入和微调）带来的隐私泄露风险：模型在适应专业术语后，可能被诱导转录出上下文或训练数据中发音相似的敏感词，即使实际发音不同。实验表明两种机制均导致可测泄露，且组合使用时泄露加剧。提示级缓解策略效果有限，无上下文提示的微调在准确率-泄露权衡中表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音LLM在专业场景中的领域定制化（用户通过提示词提供敏感信息或对专有录音进行微调）已成为标准实践，但定制化带来的隐私泄露风险尚未被系统研究。

Method: 构建受控数据集，分别测量基于提示词（prompting）和微调（fine-tuning）两种定制化机制下的信息泄露率，并评估两者组合时的叠加效应及提示级缓解策略的效果。

Result: 提示词和微调均导致可测量的隐私泄露，两者组合时泄露加剧；提示级缓解策略效果有限；无上下文提示的微调在准确率与泄露之间达到最佳平衡。

Conclusion: 语音LLM的领域定制化存在被利用来泄露隐私信息的风险，需在定制化方法与隐私保护之间进行权衡，无上下文提示的微调是相对更优的选择。

Abstract: SpeechLLMs are increasingly deployed in professional settings where domain customisation is standard practice: users supply context in prompts with sensitive information, fine-tune on proprietary recordings, or both. We identify and systematically investigate an overlooked privacy risk of such customisation: a model adapted to recognise domain-specific terminology can be nudged into transcribing a phonetically similar word from its context or training data, even when a different word is spoken, thereby leaking private information. To evaluate this risk, we construct a controlled dataset and measure leakage rates across two customisation mechanisms, prompting and fine-tuning. Both mechanisms cause measurable leakage, compounding when combined. We evaluate a prompt-level mitigation strategy and analyse the accuracy-leakage trade-off across customisation approaches, finding that fine-tuning without context prompts offers the best balance. We release our code and dataset publicly.

</details>


### [168] [Analyzing Quality-Latency-Resource Trade-offs in a Technical Documentation RAG Assistant Using LoRA Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28222)
*Evgenii Palnikov,Elizaveta Gavrilova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在基于文档的检索增强生成（RAG）系统中，系统研究了使用LoRA（低秩适应）微调生成器时的质量-延迟-资源权衡。通过5,144个Kubernetes官方文档QA对构建基准，结合固定混合检索流程，对Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct在20种LoRA配置下进行消融实验，发现仅作用于q和v注意力投影的LoRA适配器在帕累托前沿中持续占优，且3B/8B模型选择主要决定运行体制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统研究多关注检索环节优化，对生成器微调（尤其是参数高效微调方法如LoRA）在质量、延迟、资源三者间的系统权衡缺乏深入理解。作者希望填补这一空白，为实际部署提供指导。

Method: 构建了5,144个Kubernetes官方文档QA对的人工验证基准；采用固定混合检索流程（BGE-M3稠密+稀疏检索、倒数秩融合、交叉编码器重排序）；在Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上对20种LoRA配置（不同秩和目标模块选择）进行消融；评估指标包括token级F1、LLM判断的groundedness和correctness (pass@4)、推理延迟、推理内存和训练成本，所有指标均附bootstrap 95%置信区间；通过帕累托分析和参数匹配对照实验进行分析。

Result: 仅作用于q和v注意力投影的LoRA适配器在帕累托前沿中持续占优；3B/8B模型选择主要决定运行体制（operating regime）；参数匹配对照实验表明q/v优势是结构性的而非纯参数数量优势。

Conclusion: 在RAG场景下，选择仅适配q和v投影的LoRA配置能在质量、延迟和资源间达到最优权衡；模型规模选择（3B vs 8B）决定了性能-成本的基本边界；该结论为实际部署RAG+LoRA系统提供了明确的配置指导。

Abstract: We study quality-latency-resource trade-offs in a documentation-grounded retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) system that uses Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) of the generator. We build a manually verified benchmark of 5,144 question-answer pairs over the official Kubernetes documentation and combine it with a fixed hybrid-retrieval pipeline (BGE-M3 dense, BGE-M3 native sparse, Reciprocal Rank Fusion, cross-encoder reranking). Over this benchmark we ablate 20 LoRA configurations on Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct across rank and target-module choices, and evaluate each on token-level F1, LLM-judged groundedness and correctness (pass@4), inference latency, inference memory, and training cost, all reported with bootstrap 95% confidence intervals. Pareto analysis shows that LoRA adapters acting only on the q and v attention projections consistently dominate the front, while the 3B/8B choice mainly defines operating regime. A param-matched control comparison further indicates that the q/v advantage is structural rather than purely parametric. The benchmark, selected adapters, and code are available at https://github.com/EugPal/rag-lora-tradeoffs.

</details>


### [169] [Supervised Semantic Differential for Cross-Cultural Concept Analysis: A Case Study of Human Affect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28225)
*Jan Sikora,Paweł Lenartowicz,Hubert Plisiecki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出跨语言扩展的监督语义微分法（Cross-lingual SSD），通过对齐的多语言词嵌入空间评估语义梯度，并利用排列检验和自助法区间测试梯度对齐与差异。在波兰语、英语、法语情感规范词典上的实验表明，情感维度（Valence、Arousal、Dominance）在跨语言中显著可恢复，Valence高度共享，而Arousal和Dominance呈现结构化差异（如身体威胁、审美刺激、内在情绪性等），同时部分聚类反映语料库伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨文化心理学比较需要超越词汇级翻译的方法，以考察语义维度在不同语言中的组织方式。现有方法难以系统量化跨语言语义空间的异同。

Method: 1) 跨语言扩展的监督语义微分法（Cross-lingual SSD），在已对齐的多语言词嵌入空间中估计监督语义梯度；2) 使用排列检验和自助法置信区间测试梯度对齐与差异；3) 通过围绕差异梯度的聚类解释残差差异。

Result: 情感维度在跨语言中显著可恢复；Valence跨语言高度共享；Arousal和Dominance呈现结构化差异（如身体威胁、审美刺激、内在情绪性、宏观权威、日常控制）；部分聚类反映语料库特定伪影。

Conclusion: 跨语言SSD为测试语义对齐、识别差异以及生成跨文化心理意义差异的假设提供了一个可解释的框架，但需要谨慎解释语料库伪影的影响。

Abstract: Cross-cultural comparison of psychological meaning requires methods that go beyond word-level translation and examine how semantic dimensions are organized across languages. We introduce a cross-lingual extension of the Supervised Semantic Differential (SSD), which estimates supervised semantic gradients in embedding space and compares them across aligned multilingual word embeddings. The method tests gradient alignment and difference using permutation procedures and bootstrap intervals, and interprets residual differences through clustering around the difference gradient. We demonstrate the approach on Polish, English, and French affective norm lexicons, modeling Valence, Arousal, and Dominance where available. Affective dimensions were significantly recoverable across languages and model settings. Cross-lingual comparisons showed broad alignment together with structured residual differences: Valence appeared mostly shared, whereas Arousal and Dominance produced more interpretable contrasts involving bodily threat, aesthetic stimulation, internal emotionality, macro-level authority, and everyday control. Several clusters also reflected corpus-specific artifacts, underscoring the need for cautious interpretation. Cross-lingual SSD offers an explainable framework for testing semantic alignment, identifying divergence, and generating hypotheses about cross-cultural differences in psychological meaning.

</details>


### [170] [Why We Need Speech to Evaluate Speech Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28227)
*Maike Züfle,Danni Liu,Vilém Zouhar,Jan Niehues*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文元评估了文本和语音质量估计指标在性别一致性和韵律两个对比数据集上的表现，发现两者均存在不足。作者训练了SpeechCOMET模型家族并评估了SpeechLLM作为裁判，两者在标准质量估计上达到或超过文本COMET，但均未能一致评估语音特定现象。原因有三：语音特征未可靠保留、模型倾向于忽略语音源信号、训练数据中相关样本过少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音翻译模型越来越能保留说话人性别、韵律、重音等语音特有信息，但现有的评估指标对此类现象视而不见。

Method: (1) 在性别一致性和韵律两个对比数据集上元评估文本和语音质量估计指标；(2) 训练包含语音编码器的SpeechCOMET质量估计模型家族；(3) 评估SOTA SpeechLLM作为裁判；(4) 分析失败原因：语音特征编码不可靠、模型忽略语音源信号、训练数据中相关样本不足。

Result: 文本和语音指标均无法可靠评估语音特有现象；SpeechCOMET和SpeechLLM在标准质量估计上表现良好，但在语音特定评估上均不一致。

Conclusion: 在语音翻译质量评估中，标准指标无法感知语音特有信息；需要专门的语音特定训练数据和真正以语音为条件的模型才能取得进展。

Abstract: Speech translation models are increasingly capable of preserving speech-specific information (e.g., speaker gender, prosody, and emphasis), yet evaluation metrics remain blind to such phenomena. We meta-evaluate both text- and speech-based quality estimation metrics on two contrastive datasets targeting gender agreement and prosody, and find that both fall short, even when given direct access to the speech signal. We then train SpeechCOMET, a family of quality estimation models with speech encoders, and evaluate a state-of-the-art SpeechLLM as a judge. Both match or exceed text-based COMET on standard quality estimation, but neither consistently assesses speech-specific phenomena. We identify three causes: (1) speech-specific features are not reliably preserved in current encoders, (2) models tend to ignore the speech source signal, and (3) quality estimation training data contains too few relevant examples. We release all models and code, and argue that progress requires dedicated speech-specific training data and models that genuinely condition on speech.

</details>


### [171] [When Seekers Are Hard to Help: Evaluating Emotional Support Dialogue Systems in Worst-Case Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28228)
*Jiajie Yang,Yangchun Li,Guanyi Chen,Rui Fan,Xin Bai,Tingting He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究情感支持对话系统在 worst-case 交互（如低参与度、抗拒、情感波动等困难求助者）下的评估，通过专家模拟研究识别关键局限，提出包含 LLM 模拟器和四项指标的评估框架，发现现有系统在困难场景下性能大幅下降，并证明 worst-case 模拟可生成有用训练数据提升小模型鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感支持对话系统通常使用 LLM 模拟的"合作型"求助者进行评估，这类模拟用户行为理想化（清晰表达、积极回应、易接受帮助），导致评估结果过于乐观，无法反映系统处理真实困难求助场景的能力。

Method: 首先邀请 8 位资深心理咨询师进行专家模拟研究，模拟困难求助者与现有中文 ESDS 交互，完成量表评分和半结构化访谈；基于此提取 worst-case 求助者行为模式及系统局限；提出包含 LLM 驱动的 worst-case 求助者模拟器和四项指标（深度情感理解、引导式探索、平衡的情感支持、真实可靠的支持）的评估框架；在 17 个系统上评估并验证 worst-case 模拟数据可用于训练。

Result: 几乎所有模型在 worst-case 交互下性能大幅下降；大型通用 LLM 通常比专用 ESDS 更鲁棒，但即使最强模型也难以维持参与度并改善求助者情绪状态；worst-case 模拟数据可有效提升小模型鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 当前情感支持对话系统在应对困难求助场景时存在显著不足，需要更严格的 worst-case 评估方法，而 worst-case 模拟不仅用于评估还可用于训练，为提升 ESDS 在实际场景中的可靠性提供了新方向。

Abstract: Emotional Support Dialogue Systems (ESDSes) are increasingly evaluated and trained with LLM-simulated seekers. However, such simulated seekers often behave as cooperative, average-case users who disclose clearly, respond constructively, and accept support within a few turns. This can lead to overly optimistic evaluation and obscure whether ESDSes can handle difficult help-seeking interactions. In this work, we study ESDS evaluation under worst-case interactions, where seekers are hard to help due to low engagement, resistance, limited self-disclosure, emotional volatility, or rigid negative interpretations. We first conduct an expert simulation study with eight experienced counselling professionals, who simulate difficult seekers, interact with existing Chinese ESDSes, provide scale ratings, and participate in semi-structured interviews. Based on this study, we derive worst-case seeker behaviours and identify key limitations of current systems. We then propose a worst-case evaluation framework consisting of an LLM-based worst-case seeker simulator and four worst-case-oriented metrics: Deep Emotional Understanding, Guided Exploration, Balanced Emotional Support, and Authentic and Grounded Support. Evaluating 17 systems, we find that nearly all models suffer substantial performance drops under worst-case interactions. Large general-purpose LLMs are generally more robust than specialised ESDSes, but even the strongest models struggle to sustain engagement and improve seekers' emotional states. Finally, we show that worst-case simulation can also generate useful training data, improving the robustness of smaller models.

</details>


### [172] [Building Community-Centred NLP Resources for Puno Quechua](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28253)
*Elwin Huaman,Adrian Gamarra Lafuente,Johanna Cordova,Anna Korhonen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文为普诺克丘亚语（濒危语言）构建了首个专用ASR资源：66小时语音语料库（含36小时人工标注）、系统化ASR基准测试，并开源了所有数据集和微调模型（Whisper-base, wav2vec2-base, XLS-R-300M）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱势语言的数字化保护需要由母语者参与构建的数字工具和资源，而普诺克丘亚语缺乏专用的ASR资源。

Method: 通过参与式设计活动采集了66小时语音数据（脚本语音和自发语音），其中36小时经过人工转写和验证；基于Whisper-base、wav2vec2-base和XLS-R-300M进行微调，并探索了继续预训练（CPT）策略。

Result: 构建了最大的单一克丘亚语变体语音语料库（66小时），建立了首个系统化ASR基准，并开源了全部数据和模型。

Conclusion: 本文为普诺克丘亚语的语音识别奠定了数据与模型基础，其参与式收集和开源发布模式可为其他弱势语言保护提供参考。

Abstract: The preservation of under-resourced languages requires digital tools and resources shaped by and for their speakers. We present the first dedicated ASR resources for Puno Quechua (ISO 639-3: qxp): (1) the largest speech corpus for any single Quechua variety, consisting in 66 hours of recordings for scripted and spontaneous speech (including 36 hours of manually transcribed and validated data), collected via a participatory design campaign; (2) the first systematic ASR benchmark for Puno Quechua, evaluating state-of-the-art models and fine-tuning Whisper-base, wav2vec2-base, and XLS-R-300M, with and without continued pre-training (CPT); (3) an open release of all datasets and fine-tuned models.

</details>


### [173] [PrunePath: Towards Highly Structured Sparse Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28283)
*Zhexuan Gu,Zixun Fu,Yancheng Yuan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PrunePath是一种预算自适应的结构化稀疏框架，通过软最大化归一化路由分布和累积质量阈值实现FFN层的稀疏化，在NLU、NLG和指令微调任务中取得优于现有方法的稀疏-性能权衡，并利用Triton内核将结构化稀疏转化为实际的解码速度提升和内存节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有剪枝方法难以将稀疏性转化为硬件友好的推理效率提升，FFN层占据了现代语言模型的大部分参数和计算量。

Method: 基于MoEfication，将独立专家级阈值替换为软最大化归一化路由分布，并在累积质量阈值下激活重要专家，实现令牌级概率预算和自适应专家数量控制。

Result: 在NLU、NLG和指令微调评估中，与静态剪枝和基于MoEfication的方法相比，取得了更优的稀疏-性能权衡；通过Triton内核实现了KV缓存解码的实际内存节省和速度提升。

Conclusion: PrunePath为构建高度稀疏且易于部署的大语言模型提供了优越的性能。

Abstract: Feed-forward networks (FFNs) dominate the parameter count and computation of modern language models, yet existing pruning methods often struggle to convert sparsity into hardware-friendly inference efficiency gains. We introduce \textbf{PrunePath}, a budget-adaptive structured sparsification framework for FFN layers. Built on MoEfication, PrunePath replaces independent expert-wise thresholding with a softmax-normalized routing distribution and activates important experts under a cumulative-mass threshold. This formulation imposes a token-level probability budget, enabling adaptive expert counts and a direct inference-time sparsity knob from a single checkpoint. Across NLU, NLG, and instruction-tuning evaluations, PrunePath achieves a favorable sparsity--performance trade-off compared with existing static pruning and MoEfication-based methods. We further implement Triton kernels for KV-cache decoding to translate the resulting structured sparsity into practical memory savings and measurable decoding-speed improvements. These results demonstrate the superior performance of PrunePath for building highly sparse, deployment-friendly large language models.

</details>


### [174] [CIRF: Tokenizing Chain-of-Thoughts into Reusable Functional Units for Efficient Latent Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28292)
*Yukyung Lee,Yumeng Shen,Jinhyeong Park,Hyein Yang,Jun-Hyung Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CIRF是一种隐式思维链框架，通过将显式推理分解为可重用的离散功能令牌，实现了推理的并行训练、显式对齐和自适应推理，在多个推理基准上取得了优于现有隐式CoT方法的准确率-延迟权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐式CoT方法缺乏与显式推理过程的对齐，且无法根据样例复杂度自适应调整推理深度，导致推理效率与效果难以兼顾。

Method: CIRF将显式CoT轨迹中的每个语义连贯推理单元分配一个功能令牌，随后微调模型以自回归方式生成功能令牌及其可选结果，最后输出最终答案。隐式推理过程被建模为一系列离散功能令牌的动态序列。

Result: 在数学、符号和常识推理基准上的实验表明，CIRF相比现有隐式CoT方法在准确率-延迟权衡上表现更优；进一步分析显示CIRF构建了可区分的、可解释的功能令牌，带来了持续的性能提升。

Conclusion: CIRF通过将隐式推理结构化为离散功能令牌序列，有效实现了与显式推理的对齐和自适应推理，为隐式CoT提供了一种兼顾效率与效果的解决方案。

Abstract: Implicit Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reduces the inference cost of large language models by internalizing the explicit rationales. However, existing approaches typically lack alignment with explicit rationales and adaptivity to example complexity. In this work, we propose CIRF (\textit{\underline{C}hain-of-thoughts \underline{I}nto \underline{R}eusable \underline{F}unctional units}), an implicit CoT framework that performs reasoning as a dynamic sequence of discrete functional tokens. CIRF assigns a functional token to each semantically coherent reasoning unit in explicit CoT traces. The model is then fine-tuned to autoregressively generate functional tokens and their optional results, followed by the final answer. This design aligns latent reasoning with a sequence of functional units, facilitating parallel training, explicit rationale alignment, and adaptive reasoning. Extensive experiments on mathematical, symbolic, and commonsense reasoning benchmarks show that CIRF provides a favorable accuracy-latency trade-off compared with state-of-the-art implicit CoT methods. Further analyses demonstrate that CIRF constructs distinct, interpretable functional tokens, leading to consistent performance improvements.

</details>


### [175] [Routing-Aligned Fine-Tuning for Multilingual Downstream Tasks in Mixture-of-Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28306)
*Guanzhi Deng,Kuan Wu,Haibo Wang,Shing Yin Wong,Sichun Luo,Linqi Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出RA-MoE框架，通过识别MoE模型中间层的语言通用对齐区域，利用路由对齐损失进行微调，在多语言下游任务上提升效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE模型微调方法将模型视为整体学习者，忽略了预训练中形成的异构路由结构，导致非英语下游任务适应困难。

Method: 三阶段框架：(1) 基于英语和目标语言正确性将并行任务示例分为四类(cc/ci/ic/ii)；(2) 识别中间层任务相关专家；(3) 在标准SFT基础上增加路由对齐损失，引导ci类示例的目标语言路由跟随英语任务专家激活模式。

Result: 在三种MoE模型、三项任务和六种目标语言上，RA-MoE一致优于标准SFT及Routing Steering、RISE等强基线方法。

Conclusion: 中间层存在语言通用对齐区域，路由分歧可预测语言间性能差距；ci比例可作为对齐收益的可靠预测指标。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have emerged as a dominant paradigm for efficient LLM scaling, yet adapting them to non-English downstream tasks remains challenging. Existing fine-tuning approaches treat MoE models as monolithic learners, ignoring the heterogeneous routing structure that develops during pretraining. We validate across multiple MoE models and downstream tasks that middle layers form a language-universal alignment zone where routing divergence strongly predicts per-language task performance gaps. Building on this observation, we propose RA-MoE (Routing-Aligned MoE Fine-Tuning), a three-stage framework that categorizes parallel task examples into a four-way taxonomy (cc/ci/ic/ii) based on correctness in English and the target language, identifies task-relevant experts in the middle layers, and augments standard SFT with a routing alignment loss that encourages target-language routing on ci-type examples to follow the English task-expert activation pattern. Experiments across three MoE models, three tasks, and six target languages demonstrate that RA-MoE consistently outperforms standard SFT and strong baselines including Routing Steering and RISE, with the ci proportion of a task-language pair serving as a reliable predictor of alignment benefit.

</details>


### [176] [HELEA: Hard-Negative Benchmark and LLM-based Reranking for Robust Entity Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28308)
*Yoonjin Jang,Junwoo Kim,Youngjoong Ko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出同名校对的硬负例增强策略，构建了质量可控的评估基准（DW-HN29K, DY-HN27K）和增强训练语料，并设计了两阶段框架HELEA（实体编码器检索+LLM重排序），解决了现有实体对齐基准中名称重叠导致评测失效的问题，在硬负例基准上实现F1 0.967，同时保持标准基准上Hit@1 0.993。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实体对齐基准存在名称重叠问题，使得模型可利用名称匹配而非关系结构进行对齐，且缺乏评估模型能否拒绝同名但指代不同真实对象的实体的能力。

Method: (1) 从KG名称冲突组中挖掘同名但不同的实体对，构建同名校对硬负例增强策略，生成质量可控的基准（DW-HN29K, DY-HN27K）和增强训练语料（DW-Train, DY-Train）；(2) 提出HELEA两阶段框架：阶段一利用硬负例增强训练语料和1跳KG上下文训练实体编码器检索，阶段二使用LLM进行无需额外训练的重排序。

Result: 依赖名称的基线模型在硬负例基准上性能骤降至接近随机水平；HELEA在DW-HN29K上达到F1 0.967，同时在标准DW-15K上保持Hit@1 0.993。

Conclusion: 硬负例增强策略有效暴露了名称依赖模型的缺陷，HELEA通过结合硬负例训练的检索器与LLM重排序，在保持标准对齐性能的同时显著提升了硬负例场景下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Entity Alignment (EA) is essential for knowledge graph (KG) fusion, but existing benchmarks often allow models to exploit name overlap rather than relational structure. This makes it difficult to evaluate whether models can reject same-name entities that refer to different real-world objects. Our primary contribution is a same-name hard-negative augmentation strategy that simultaneously yields quality-controlled evaluation benchmarks (DW-HN29K, DY-HN27K) and augmented training corpora (DW-Train, DY-Train), by mining same-name but distinct entity pairs from KG name-collision groups. We further introduce HELEA, a two-stage framework integrating (i) entity encoder retrieval trained on hard-negative-augmented training corpora with 1-hop KG context, and (ii) LLM-based reranking without additional training. Experiments show that name-dependent baselines collapse to near-random performance on our hard-negative benchmarks, while HELEA achieves F1 0.967 on DW-HN29K while maintaining Hit@1 0.993 on standard DW-15K.

</details>


### [177] [Argument Quality Assessment with Large Language Models: A Pairwise Bradley-Terry Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28313)
*Nicolás Benjamín Ocampo,Agnes Paullate Nyiranziza,Davide Ceolin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了大型语言模型（LLMs）在论证质量评估中的表现。作者测试了12个开源LLM，在零样本、少样本和思维链条件下进行论证质量的成对比较，覆盖逻辑、修辞和辩证三个维度，并使用Bradley-Terry模型推断潜在强度评分。结果表明，LLMs与人类专家判断具有中等相关性，其中Llama-70B表现最佳（Cohen's κ=0.493），其他模型与Llama-70B的对齐程度从弱到强不等。LLM预测在试验间具有稳定性（标签变异<7.75%），剩余变异通过多数投票和少样本提示处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论证质量评估需要严格的评价标准，而LLMs在推理和判断任务中展现了出色能力，因此研究者希望探究LLMs在多大程度上能有效完成论证质量评估这一复杂任务。

Method: 测试了12个不同规模和家族的开源LLM，在零样本、少样本和思维链条件下进行论证质量的成对比较，覆盖逻辑、修辞和辩证三个维度。使用Bradley-Terry模型从成对比较中推断潜在强度分数并推导论证排序，通过与人类专家判断的相关性分析评估模型表现。

Result: LLMs与人类专家判断具有中等相关性，Llama-70B表现最佳（Cohen's κ=0.493，与Bradley-Terry得分的Kendall、Pearson和Spearman相关系数为0.327-0.477）。其他模型与Llama-70B的对齐程度从弱到强不等，但在与人类专家的比较中取得了可比结果。LLM预测稳定性高，标签变异低于7.75%。

Conclusion: LLMs在论证质量评估方面展现出有前景但中等程度的与人类专家判断的相关性，不同规模和家族的模型对质量维度的理解具有部分互补性。多数投票和少样本提示可有效处理剩余变异，尤其适用于大规模模型。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in tasks related to reasoning and judgment. However, assessing the quality of arguments requires a rigorous evaluation. We investigate the extent to which LLMs can effectively perform this task. We tested 12 open-weight LLMs of different sizes and families under zero-shot, few-shot, and chain-of-thought to approximate expert pairwise comparisons of argument quality across three dimensions-logical, rhetorical, and dialectic-and used these comparisons in a Bradley-Terry model to infer latent strength scores and derive a ranking of arguments. Our insights show that LLMs have promising but moderate correlation with human expert judgments, with Llama-70B obtaining the strongest alignment, reaching moderate Cohen's $κ$ = 0.493 and moderate correlations with Bradley-Terry scores derived from these annotations (Kendall, Pearson, and Spearman: 0.327-0.477). Other LLMs exhibit weak, moderate, or high alignment with Llama-70B while achieving comparable results against human experts, suggesting partial but complementary understanding of underlying quality dimensions despite differences in model size and family. Moreover, LLM predictions are stable across trial runs, with fewer than 7.75\% of cases yielding different labels. Remaining variability is handled via majority voting and few-shot prompting for large-size models.

</details>


### [178] [HardMTBench: Stress-Testing Chinese-English Translation on Knowledge-Intensive Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28315)
*Zheng Li,Mao Zheng,Mingyang Song,Tianxiang Fei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对中英双向翻译基准FLORES-200已趋于饱和的问题，本文提出HardMTBench——一个难度感知的诊断性基准，涵盖12个领域、10,000条手工筛选源句（共20,000个测试项）。通过三阶段构建流程（候选池构建、LLM多信号难度评估、难度融合规则分配）生成测试集。在22个系统上的实验表明，HardMTBench将系统间GEMBA分数差距扩大约2倍（相比FLORES-200），引发明显的排名重排序，并暴露出领域特定术语和知识上的弱点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用机器翻译基准（如FLORES-200）在中英方向上已趋于饱和，各系统得分差距极小（标准差仅2.29），无法有效区分不同系统在知识密集型领域（金融、医疗、法律、科技等）的翻译能力。

Method: 三阶段构建流程：(1) 构建覆盖12个领域、84,566对的领域平衡候选池；(2) 采用基于LLM的多信号评估器，对知识密度、翻译难度、术语负载和参考译文正确性进行评分；(3) 通过难度融合规则，按领域配额组装最终含10,000条源句（20,000个测试项）的测试集。

Result: 在22个系统（涵盖通用大语言模型、商业翻译引擎和专门翻译模型）上的实验显示：HardMTBench将系统间GEMBA分数范围扩大约2倍（相比FLORES-200），导致显著的排名重排序，并能暴露仅靠质量指标难以区分的领域术语和知识弱点。

Conclusion: HardMTBench作为难度感知的诊断性基准，能更有效地评估和区分不同翻译系统在知识密集型领域中的实际能力，弥补了现有基准的区分度不足问题。数据和代码均已开源。

Abstract: General-purpose machine translation benchmarks such as FLORES-200 have reached a saturation regime on Chinese-English pairs, where modern large language models cluster within a narrow band of high scores. Across 22 systems, FLORES-200 zh-en GEMBA scores fall in a 7.87-point range with a standard deviation of 2.29, which compresses the separation between systems on knowledge-intensive domains such as finance, healthcare, law, and science and technology. We introduce HardMTBench, a difficulty-aware diagnostic benchmark for bidirectional Chinese-English domain translation. HardMTBench covers 12 domains and contains 10,000 hand-curated source sentences with reference translations, packaged as 20,000 directional test items. A three-stage construction pipeline builds a domain-balanced candidate pool of 84{,}566 pairs, applies an LLM-based multi-signal judge over knowledge density, translation difficulty, terminology load and reference correctness, and assembles the final test set under a hardness fusion rule with per-domain quotas. Across 22 systems spanning general LLMs, commercial engines and specialised MT models, HardMTBench widens the cross-system GEMBA range by roughly a factor of two over FLORES-200, induces visible rank reorderings, and exposes domain-specific terminology and knowledge weaknesses that quality-only metrics tend to flatten. All data and code are open-sourced at https://github.com/jasonNLP/HardMTBench.

</details>


### [179] [When Discourse Pressures Conflict: Information Structure in Vision-Language Model Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28346)
*Marcell Fekete,Johannes Bjerva,Tamás Káldi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 视觉语言模型（VLM）在识别正确视觉内容方面表现良好，但在语篇适切性上存在不足。通过利用匈牙利语中话题（Topic）与焦点（Focus）映射到特定句法位置的特点，研究比较了六种VLM与人类在信息结构（IS）选择上的差异，发现模型虽能产生IS相关结构但过度正则化，倾向于使用固定响应模板，而人类则采用灵活策略。建议VLM评估应超越内容准确性，关注内容的语篇包装方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM评估主要关注视觉内容的准确性，但忽略了模型是否以语篇适切的形式表达内容。本文旨在填补这一研究空白，探究VLM在视觉问答中是否能够区分语篇已知的话题与新信息焦点。

Method: 利用匈牙利语中话题与焦点映射到特定句法位置的语言特性，通过对比六种VLM与人类参与者在视觉问答任务中的信息结构选择，分析模型在语篇状态、语法角色（主语偏好话题）和限定性（不定指偏好焦点）多重压力下的表现。

Result: VLM能够生成与信息结构相关的构式，但表现出过度正则化的敏感性。与人类灵活多样的信息结构实现策略不同，模型倾向于坍缩到狭窄的响应模板，类似于模式坍缩（mode collapse）。

Conclusion: VLM评估应超越内容准确性，关注内容在语篇中的包装方式（即信息结构的适切性），因为模型在语篇适切性方面与人类存在显著差距。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly evaluated for whether they identify the right visual content, but little is known about whether they express such content in a discourse-appropriate form. We address this research gap using information structure (IS), testing whether VLMs distinguish discourse-old Topics from discourse-new Foci in visually grounded question answering. We exploit Hungarian, a language in which Topic and Focus map onto dedicated syntactic positions, making IS choices observable in text. Comparing six VLMs with human participants, we find that models produce IS-relevant constructions, but over-regularise this sensitivity. Under the interacting pressures of discourse status, grammatical role (preference for subject Topics) and definiteness (preference for indefinite Foci), humans choose variable strategies for IS realisation. VLMs, by contrast, collapse onto narrow response templates, resembling mode collapse (Kirk et al., 2024). Our findings suggest that VLM evaluation should look beyond content accuracy to how content is packaged for the discourse.

</details>


### [180] [PubMedCausal: A Span-Level Annotated Corpus for Causal Relation Extraction in Biomedical Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28363)
*Ifeoluwa Kunle-John,Josiah Paul,Oluwatosin Agbaakin,Peter Aina,Ikenna Odezuligbo,Sydney Anuyah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PubMedCausal是一个基于PubMed摘要构建的跨度级标注语料库，包含30,000个段落级行、3,945个因果行和6,491个因果对，支持因果检测和完整跨度因果抽取的评估。基准测试显示PubMedBERT在因果检测中F1达0.7391，DeepSeek-R1-32B在跨度抽取中Cosine Pair F1达0.6765，研究揭示了类别不平衡、长因果跨度、隐式因果关系等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物医学因果关系抽取资源存在三大局限：常将因果关系与更广泛的关联混为一谈、仅限句子级标注、主要关注显式因果线索，无法评估模型在真实生物医学文本中恢复因果声明的能力。因此需要构建更全面的跨度级标注语料库。

Method: 从PubMed摘要构建包含30,000段落级行、3,945因果行和6,491因果对的跨度级标注语料库，标注完整文本因果跨度、因果类型和句属性。在因果检测和跨度抽取两方面基准测试判别式编码器和开源生成模型，并评估跨数据集迁移学习。

Result: 因果检测中PubMedBERT最优（F1=0.7391）；跨度抽取中DeepSeek-R1-32B搭配少样本提示最优（Cosine Pair F1=0.6765）。迁移学习表明PubMedCausal训练的编码器可支持跨数据集评估。生物医学CRE在类别不平衡、长因果跨度、隐式因果、跨句关系和提示敏感性等场景下仍具挑战。

Conclusion: PubMedCausal作为面向真实生物医学文本的因果关系抽取资源，填补了现有语料库的空白，基准测试显示当前模型在该任务上仍面临显著挑战，为未来研究提供了方向和评估基准。

Abstract: Causal relation extraction (CRE) is central to biomedical text mining, but current resources often conflate causal relations with broader associations, restrict annotation to sentence-level examples, or focus mainly on explicit causal cues. This limits their usefulness for evaluating whether models can recover causal claims as they are actually expressed in biomedical text. We introduce PubMedCausal, a span-level annotated corpus for biomedical CRE built from PubMed abstracts. The corpus contains 30,000 paragraph-level rows, including 3,945 causal rows and 6,491 adjudicated cause--effect pairs. Each causal relation is annotated with full-text cause and effect spans, causality type, and sententiality, enabling evaluation of both causal detection and full-span causal extraction. We benchmark discriminative encoders and open-source generative models across detection and extraction settings. For causal detection, biomedical encoders are strongest, with PubMedBERT reaching an F$_1$ score of 0.7391. For span-level extraction, the best generative baseline is DeepSeek-R1-32B with few-shot prompting, reaching a Cosine Pair F$_1$ of 0.6765. We further test transfer learning by evaluating PubMedCausal-trained encoders on external causal relation datasets, showing that the resource supports cross-dataset evaluation. Our results show that biomedical CRE remains difficult under class imbalance, long causal spans, implicit causality, inter-sentential relations, and prompt sensitivity. Code and Data can be found here: https://github.com/josiahpaul07/PubMedCausal_Exp

</details>


### [181] [PrionNER: A Named Entity Recognition Dataset for Prion Disease Biomedical Literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28375)
*An Dao,Nhan Ly,Thao Tran,Yuji Matsumoto,Akiko Aizawa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍PrionNER，一个针对朊病毒疾病临床信息的手动标注命名实体识别数据集，涵盖317篇PubMed摘要、2943个句子、6955个实体标注，包含15种粗粒度和31种细粒度临床实体类型。基准测试表明W2NER为最优监督模型，Gemma-4-31B为最优零样本模型，但任务仍具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 朊病毒疾病是罕见、快速进展且致命的神经退行性疾病，早期诊断困难，且缺乏公开可用的、专门针对该疾病临床信息的标注数据集。作者旨在填补这一空白，为罕见病生物医学信息提取提供低资源、细粒度、非平展标注条件下的基准。

Method: 构建了PrionNER数据集：从PubMed摘要中手动标注317篇摘要、2943个句子、6955个实体，涵盖15种粗粒度和31种细粒度实体类型（疾病、症状、诊断、检查发现、解剖、治疗、时间及统计证据等）。采用监督BERT基线、W2NER和零样本抽取器进行基准测试。

Result: 标注者间一致性达到81.78%精确匹配F1值，表明标注一致性高。W2NER为最强监督模型，Gemma-4-31B为最强零样本模型。但基准测试仍有挑战，特别是在结构复杂的提及和细粒度临床相邻标签区分方面。

Conclusion: PrionNER为朊病毒疾病信息提取提供了临床导向的基准，支持罕见病生物医学NLP研究。数据集、标注指南和评估脚本已公开在GitHub上。

Abstract: Prion diseases are rare, rapidly progressive, and fatal neurodegenerative disorders that remain difficult to diagnose, particularly in their early stages because of nonspecific clinical presentations. However, to our knowledge, there is no publicly available prion-disease-focused dataset designed to capture a broad range of clinically relevant entities from the biomedical literature. We introduce PrionNER, a manually annotated named entity recognition dataset for prion disease clinical information in PubMed abstracts. The current release comprises 317 abstracts, 2,943 sentences, and 6,955 text-bound entity annotations spanning 15 coarse-grained and 31 fine-grained clinically oriented entity types covering diseases, symptoms, diagnostics, findings, anatomy, treatments, and temporal and statistical evidence. Inter-annotator agreement reaches 81.78 exact-match F1, indicating strong annotation consistency. We benchmark supervised BERT baselines, W2NER, and zero-shot extractors on PrionNER. W2NER is the strongest supervised model, and Gemma-4-31B is the strongest zero-shot model, but the benchmark remains challenging, especially for structurally complex mentions and fine-grained clinically adjacent label distinctions. PrionNER provides a clinically grounded benchmark for prion-disease information extraction and supports research on rare-disease biomedical NLP under low-resource, fine-grained, and non-flat extraction conditions. The dataset, annotation guidelines, and evaluation scripts are available at https://github.com/daotuanan/PrionNER/.

</details>


### [182] [FABSVer: Faster Training and Better Self-Verification for LLM Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28389)
*Haihui Pan,Junwei Bao,Hongfei Jiang,Yang Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FABSVer，将数学推理中的解生成与自验证融合为单一生成过程，并引入动态参考模型更新（DRMU）突破奖励收敛瓶颈，在三个模型规模上以仅51%-71%的训练时间实现优越的自验证与推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在数学推理中难以可靠判断自身解答的正确性；现有自验证方法将解生成与验证视为两个独立任务，导致训练时间大幅增加。

Method: 提出FABSVer，将解生成与自验证融合为单一生成过程，联合优化两种能力；提出DRMU（动态参考模型更新），通过更新参考模型突破奖励收敛瓶颈，实现奖励持续增长。

Result: 在数学基准测试上，FABSVer在三个模型规模下均取得优越的自验证与推理性能，训练时间仅为现有方法的51%-71%。

Conclusion: 模型获取自验证能力呈现不同的学习阶段；随着模型规模增大，验证奖励与答案奖励之间的差距显著缩小。

Abstract: While large language models have made significant progress in mathematical reasoning, they remain unreliable at judging the correctness of their own solutions.
