Existing approaches that equip models with self-verification typically treat solution generation and verification as two separate tasks, leading to substantially increased training time. In this paper, we propose FABSVer, which fuses these two tasks into a single generation pass, dramatically reducing training overhead while jointly optimizing both capabilities. We further identify a convergence bottleneck both theoretically and empirically: as training progresses, the reward reaches a plateau because the policy is constrained by a fixed reference model. To overcome this, we introduce Dynamic Reference Model Update (DRMU), which raises the reward ceiling and enables sustained reward growth. Extensive experiments on math benchmarks demonstrate that FABSVer achieves superior self-verification and reasoning performance across three model scales, while requiring only 51%--71% of the training time of existing methods. Analysis further reveals distinct learning phases in how models acquire self-verification, and that the gap between verify and answer rewards shrinks noticeably as model size increases.

</details>


### [183] [Skill0.5: Joint Skill Internalization and Utilization for Out-of-Distribution Generalization in Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28424)
*Jiapeng Zhu,Jianxiang Yu,Yibo Zhao,Chengcheng Han,Qi Gu,Xunliang Cai,Xiang Li,Weining Qian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Skill0.5提出了一种混合策略框架，通过将通用技能内化（通过特权蒸馏构建认知基础）与任务特定技能外化（通过诊断探测防止捷径）相结合，并利用动态难度感知路由器进行任务分级优化，在ALFWorld和WebShop上超越了现有强化学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于技能的强化学习方法在技能处理上存在两难：完全外化（上下文开销过大）与完全内化（过拟合与知识冲突）各有利弊，缺乏一种区分通用技能与任务特定技能的灵活处理机制。

Method: 提出Skill0.5框架：(1) 动态难度感知路由器将任务分流到不同掌握层级；(2) 针对困难任务，通过特权蒸馏内化通用技能以构建认知基础；(3) 针对简单任务，通过诊断探测惩罚捷径并强制使用特定技能。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop基准上，Skill0.5在分布内和分布外场景下均优于基于记忆和基于技能的强化学习基线方法。

Conclusion: 将通用技能内化与任务特定技能外化显式区分处理的混合策略是解决技能强化学习中上下文开销与过拟合困境的有效方法。

Abstract: Equipping large language models with explicit skills has emerged as a promising paradigm for enabling autonomous agents to solve complex tasks. Agent skills can be inherently divided into general skills for broad cognitive transfer and task-specific skills for dynamic execution. However, existing skill-based reinforcement learning (RL) methods typically force a rigid choice between full externalization, which incurs prohibitive context overhead, and full internalization, which risks overfitting and knowledge conflicts. To address this dilemma, we propose Skill0.5, a novel agentic RL framework that explicitly differentiates skill treatments by combining general skill internalization with task-specific skill utilization. Driven by a dynamic, difficulty-aware router, Skill0.5 streams tasks into distinct mastery tiers to apply tailored optimization strategies: it internalizes general skills via privileged distillation to build a cognitive foundation for hard tasks, while using diagnostic probing on easy tasks to penalize shortcuts and enforce specific skill utilization. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop demonstrate that Skill0.5 outperforms both memory-based and skill-based RL baselines, yielding performance improvements across both in-distribution and out-of-distribution scenarios.

</details>


### [184] [Breaking the Script Barrier: Enabling Automatic Alignment for PoS-based ASR Error Analysis in Non-Latin Scripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28438)
*Prasenjit K Mudi,Dahlia Devapriya,Sheetal Kalyani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种鲁棒、自动、语言无关的对齐机制，可适用于拉丁和非拉丁文字系统，支持细粒度的词性标注级ASR错误分析，并利用错误信息改进ASR训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASR评估依赖WER等聚合指标，无法捕捉错误的语言结构；细粒度分析需要准确的对齐工具，但现有工具对非拉丁文字系统不可靠。

Method: 提出一种语言无关的鲁棒自动对齐机制，适用于多种ASR架构和文字系统（拉丁/非拉丁）；结合标准词性标注器进行可扩展、可复现的PoS级错误分析；并在三种主要书写体系（元音附标文字、字母文字、辅音文字）上验证。

Result: 该方法在Abugida（泰米尔语、印地语、卡纳达语）、Alphabetic（英语、俄语、希腊语）和Abjad（阿拉伯语）等多样文字系统上实现了对齐和下游ASR错误分析，并展示了利用错误信息改善WER等指标的能力。

Conclusion: 提出的对齐机制有效弥补了非拉丁文字ASR错误细粒度分析的空白，且错误信息可反馈用于提升ASR性能。

Abstract: Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) systems are commonly evaluated using aggregate metrics such as Word Error Rate (WER), which do not capture the linguistic structure of errors. Fine-grained analysis, such as Part-of-Speech (PoS)-wise error characterization, requires accurate alignment between ASR hypotheses and reference transcriptions. However, existing alignment tools are often unreliable for languages written in non-Latin scripts. In this work, we address this gap by proposing a robust, automated, language-agnostic alignment mechanism applicable across ASR architectures and across languages written in both Latin and non-Latin scripts. This enables consistent alignment of hypotheses, references, and evaluation sequences, forming the basis for downstream linguistic analysis. Building on this, we employ standard PoS taggers to perform scalable and reproducible PoS-wise error analysis. Notably, we perform alignment and downstream ASR error analysis across three major segmented writing systems, namely, Abugida (Tamil, Hindi, Kannada), Alphabetic (English, Russian, Greek), and Abjad (Arabic). We further demonstrate how such error information can be leveraged during ASR training to improve metrics such as WER.

</details>


### [185] [AdaDPO: Self-Adaptive Direct Preference Optimization with Balanced Gradient Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28440)
*Shaolong Chen,Madalina Ciobanu,Qingqing Mao,Ritankar Das*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AdaDPO 提出了一种自适应的 DPO 变体，通过引入基于策略模型生成概率的 stop-gradient 系数来平衡正负样本的梯度幅度，从而修正 DPO 中梯度不对称问题，在 AlpacaEval 2 上优于 DPO，且可轻松集成到现有偏好对齐流程中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO 存在梯度不对称问题：损失函数抑制不偏好响应的速度远快于提升偏好响应，导致模型倾向于学习避免生成坏答案而非学习生成好答案，优化效率低下。

Method: 提出 AdaDPO，引入基于策略模型生成概率的逐偏好对 stop-gradient 系数（以参考模型概率为可选组件），强制偏好与不偏好概率的梯度幅度相等；实际实现上平衡逐 token 梯度并施加数值裁剪边界以保持稳定性，同时保留 DPO 原始超参数结构。

Result: 在 Llama-3-8B-Instruct 上使用 UltraFeedback 训练，AlpacaEval 2 评测中：81% 超参数组合下获得更高长度控制胜率（LC），全局最佳 LC 达 48.3%、原始胜率达 46.1%，88% 组合中扩大了 LC 与 WR 的差距（有效缓解长度偏差）。KL 散度、奖励边际和奖励准确率分析证实 AdaDPO 修正了梯度不平衡并实现更高效优化。

Conclusion: AdaDPO 作为纯损失层面的改进方法，无需改变数据收集或模型架构，仅需数行代码即可嵌入现有基于偏好的对齐流程；其自适应原则可泛化到 SimPO、R-DPO、IPO、CPO、ORPO 等广泛的成对对比偏好损失函数族。

Abstract: DPO has become a widely adopted alternative to RLHF for aligning LLMs with human preferences, eliminating the need for a separate reward model or RL loop. Recent theoretical analysis uncovers an asymmetric gradient behavior in DPO: the loss suppresses dispreferred responses substantially faster than it promotes preferred ones, causing the model to learn to avoid bad answers rather than to generate good ones. We propose AdaDPO, a Self-Adaptive variant of the DPO algorithm that introduces per-preference-pair, stop-gradient-based coefficients derived directly from the policy model's generation probabilities, with the reference model's probabilities as an optional component. AdaDPO is constructed to enforce equality of gradient magnitudes between preferred and dispreferred probabilities; the practical implementation balances per-token gradients and applies a numerical clipping bound for stability, while retaining DPO's original hyperparameter structure. On Llama-3-8B-Instruct trained on UltraFeedback under a SimPO similar setup, AdaDPO consistently outperforms DPO on AlpacaEval 2: it achieves higher length-controlled win rates (LC) in 81% of hyperparameter combinations, attains the global best LC (48.3%) and raw win rate (46.1%), and enlarges the LC-over-WR margin in 88% of combinations, indicating effective mitigation of length bias. Additional analyses on KL divergence, reward margin, and reward accuracy confirm that AdaDPO rectifies the gradient imbalance and yields more efficient optimization. Because it operates purely at the loss level, AdaDPO can be dropped into existing preference-based alignment pipelines without changing data collection or model architectures. The method requires only a few lines of code, and the same self-adaptive principle generalizes to a broad family of pairwise contrastive preference losses including SimPO, R-DPO, IPO, CPO, and ORPO.

</details>


### [186] [The Cases LJP Never Sees: Prosecution Decision Prediction for More Complete Criminal Liability Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28464)
*Junyu Lu,Qi Wei,Peishuo Zheng,Jie Zhang,Hui Huang,Qianru Wang,Chuan Xiao,Jianbin Qin,Shuyuan Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出起诉决策预测（PDP）任务，填补了法律判决预测（LJP）在审查起诉阶段的盲区，并构建了PDP-Bench基准，实验表明先进LLMs在PDP上表现显著差于LJP，且现有增强方法无法弥补这一差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律判决预测（LJP）仅涵盖已通过审查起诉并正式提起公诉的刑事案件，忽视了证据不足、无刑事责任或免除刑罚等案件，导致对刑事责任评估存在重大盲区。为填补这一空白，提出起诉决策预测（PDP）任务。

Method: 提出了PDP任务，将案件分类为起诉或三种不起诉决定之一；构建了包含4,630个真实中国起诉决策案例、覆盖190个罪名的PDP-Bench基准；通过大量实验评估主流LLMs的性能，并探索RLVR干预等增强方法。

Result: 最新LLMs在PDP上表现显著差于LJP；主流增强路线无法缩小性能差距；受控RLVR实验表明简单的结果奖励无法产生可泛化的PDP判别能力。

Conclusion: PDP任务揭示了法律AI在证据评估、法律归摄和价值裁量方面的更高挑战，现有方法在此任务上存在明显不足，需要针对性地开发更有效的模型和训练策略。

Abstract: Legal Judgment Prediction (LJP) has become a core benchmark for evaluating AI in the criminal legal domain, but it only sees criminal cases that have already passed prosecutorial review and been formally indicted. As a result, LJP leaves a substantial blind spot in assessing criminal liability, overlooking cases involving insufficient evidence, no criminal liability, or guilt exempted from punishment. To fill this gap, we propose \textbf{Prosecution Decision Prediction (PDP)}, the first Legal AI task built around prosecutorial review, which classifies each case into prosecution or one of three non-prosecution decisions and reflects legal AI's capabilities in evidence evaluation, legal subsumption, and value-based discretion. We further construct \textbf{PDP-Bench}, a benchmark of 4{,}630 real Chinese prosecutorial decisions spanning 190 charges. Extensive experiments show that state-of-the-art LLMs perform substantially worse on PDP than on LJP and that mainstream enhancement routes fail to close the gap. Moreover, controlled RLVR interventions show that simple outcome rewards fail to produce generalizable PDP discrimination.

</details>


### [187] [Beyond One Path: Evaluating and Enhancing Divergent Thinking in Interactive LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28465)
*Jihyeong Park,Ingeol Baek,Jeonghyun Park,Hwanhee Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MUTATE是一个评估LLM在交互式环境中发散性思维的双层次基准（路径级与动作级），发现现有模型存在即时动作固着问题，并提出通过分离发散生成与收敛选择的ReDNA方法显著提升发散推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM创造力评估仅关注单轮文本生成，无法捕捉智能体在迭代交互中的发散推理过程，且仅以成功率为评价指标忽略了有价值的偏离尝试。

Method: 构建MUTATE基准，在路径级和动作级双层次评估发散性思维；提出ReDNA方法，将无约束的发散候选生成与收敛性约束选择相分离。

Result: 前沿LLM在收敛压力下表现出即时动作固着，动作级发散性差；ReDNA在两个发散层次上显著超越现有方法，并能泛化到外部创造力环境。

Conclusion: ReDNA的成功源于增强了韧性发散推理能力而非简单环境探索，揭示了现有框架中发散性思维评估的结构性盲点。

Abstract: Divergent thinking is a core dimension of creativity, yet existing evaluations of Large Language Models (LLMs) treat them as single-turn text generations, failing to capture how an agent reasons through iterative interaction. To address this, we introduce MUTATE, an interactive benchmark designed to evaluate agentic divergent thinking at two levels: path-level, where an agent discovers multiple alternative paths to the same goal, and action-level, where individual actions require non-typical, mechanism-shifting object uses. Unlike success-only evaluations, MUTATE scores both completed paths and off-path attempts, capturing divergent reasoning that conventional success rates discard. Our experiments with frontier LLMs reveal a structural blind spot in existing frameworks: when exposed to immediate convergence pressure, they tend to fall into immediate action fixation, failing to improve action-level divergence. To overcome this, we propose ReDNA, which separates unconstrained divergent candidate generation from convergent constraint selection. ReDNA significantly outperforms prior methods across both divergence levels and generalizes effectively to an external creativity environment. We also confirm its success stems from a qualitative enhancement of resilient divergent reasoning rather than simple environmental exploration.

</details>


### [188] [Comonadic Morphophonology: A Compositional Framework for Context-Dependent Morphological Rules in Finnish](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28484)
*Yongseok Jang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Writer comonad（DeletionSet × Zipper）的形态音位规则组合框架，将每条规则建模为局部上下文到输出音段的coKleisli箭头，通过伴随态射实现长度变化规则的严格组合，在保持形式化解释的同时避免了有限状态转换器（FST）的状态爆炸问题。仅用13条coKleisli箭头即可表达Omorfi中874个延续类编码的形态音位行为（规则表示层面缩减67:1），并在UD Finnish-TDT上达到83.92%的UPOS准确率（配合外部后缀标注器达94.66%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有有限状态转换器（FST）在处理上下文相关的形态音位规则（如辅音渐变、元音和谐、所有格后缀同化）时面临乘性状态爆炸问题；神经模型虽然避免了这一问题，但无法提供规则的形式化描述。因此需要一种既能保持形式化可解释性、又能避免状态爆炸的组合框架。

Method: 将每条形态音位规则建模为从焦点局部上下文到单个输出音段的函数（类似细胞自动机的局部规则）；引入Writer comonad (DeletionSet × Zipper) 这一新的代数构造，将长度变化规则作为comonad的coKleisli箭头进行组合，其中extend操作将局部规则提升为全局变换，删除操作以幺半群动作形式累积而无需中间物化。

Result: 仅需13条coKleisli箭头即可重构Omorfi用874个延续类编码的相同形态音位行为（规则表示层面缩减67:1）；同一抽象支持双向形态学——MorphGenerator复用分析箭头实现生成；在UD Finnish-TDT上，仅用规则消歧达到83.92% UPOS准确率，配合外部后缀标注器达到94.66%。

Conclusion: 该框架为形态音位规则提供了首个兼具形式化严格组合性和实际计算效率的方案，验证了comonadic方法在计算形态学中的有效性。

Abstract: Composing finite-state transducers (FSTs) for context-dependent morphophonological rules -- consonant gradation, vowel harmony, possessive suffix assimilation -- leads to multiplicative state explosion; neural models sidestep the problem but provide no formal account of the rules themselves. We present the first framework where each morphophonological rule is a function from a focused local context to a single output segment -- the type of a local rule familiar from cellular automata -- and where length-changing rules compose as coKleisli arrows of a comonad. Our central contribution is the Writer comonad (DeletionSet x Zipper), a new algebraic construction that restores strict coKleisli compositionality for such rules: each rule is a coKleisli arrow, extend lifts it to a global transformation, and deletions accumulate as a monoid action rather than requiring intermediate materialization. As supporting evidence, thirteen coKleisli arrows provide an alternative formulation expressing the same morphophonological behaviors that Omorfi encodes via 874 continuation classes (67:1 reduction at the rule-representation level), and the same abstraction enables bidirectional morphology -- a MorphGenerator reuses the analysis arrows for generation. On UD Finnish-TDT, the system achieves 83.92% UPOS accuracy with rule-only disambiguation (94.66% with an external suffix tagger), validating the framework as a practical morphological engine.

</details>


### [189] [The Abstraction Gap in Vision-Language Causal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28779)
*Chinh Hoang,Mohammad Rashedul Hasan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种双探针方法（文本探针和链式探针）及抽象差距（AG）指标，用于区分VLM的因果推理忠实度与语言合理性。在CAGE基准（含49,500个问题、5,500张图像）上评估8个VLM，发现7个模型AG超过0.50（文本得分6-8，链式得分低于2.5），微调无法缩小差距，但一个模型实现了接近零的AG，表明能力存在于当前架构中且依赖于预训练和架构选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLM评估无法区分语言合理性和忠实因果推理，需要一种能够隔离并量化这两种属性的方法论。

Method: 提出双探针方法：文本探针（Text-Only Probe）衡量语言质量；链式探针（Chain-Text Probe）要求模型先生成显式因果链。通过抽象差距（AG）指标量化归一化性能差异。构建CAGE基准，包含49,500个问题、5,500张图像，覆盖Pearl因果层次。

Result: 8个VLM中7个的AG超过0.50（文本得分6-8，链式得分低于2.5）。对45,000个链式标注样本进行微调未能缩小差距，但一个模型实现了接近零的AG。

Conclusion: 忠实因果推理能力存在于当前VLM架构中，但依赖于预训练和架构选择。CAGE可作为评估VLM忠实因果推理的诊断工具。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) generate fluent causal explanations, but current evaluations cannot distinguish linguistic plausibility from faithful causal reasoning. We introduce a dual-probe methodology that isolates these properties. The Text-Only Probe measures linguistic quality. The Chain-Text Probe requires models to first generate explicit causal chains. The Abstraction Gap (AG) metric quantifies the normalized performance difference. Evaluating eight VLMs on CAGE (Causal Abstraction Gap Evaluation), a benchmark of 49,500 questions across 5,500 images spanning Pearl's causal hierarchy, we find seven models exhibit AG exceeding 0.50 with text scores of 6--8 but chain scores below 2.5. Fine-tuning on 45,000 chain-annotated examples fails to close the gap. However, one model achieves near-zero AG. The capability exists within current VLM architectures and depends on pretraining and architectural choices. CAGE provides a diagnostic tool for assessing faithful causal reasoning in VLMs.

</details>


### [190] [A new semantically annotated corpus with syntactic-semantic and cross-lingual senses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28494)
*Myriam Rakho,Eric Laporte,Matthieu Constant*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个用于词义消歧的新型法语动词义标注语料库，包含20个多义法语动词，每个动词实例标注了三个层次的义标签：英语翻译、词典条目及二者的组合标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为法语词义消歧任务提供高质量的标注数据资源，解决多义词在平行语料与词典资源中的义项对齐问题。

Method: 从平行语料中提取20个法语多义动词的实例，对每个实例进行三重标注：(1) 该动词在平行语料英语译文中的实际翻译；(2) 法语计算词典（Lexicon-Grammar表）中的词典条目；(3) 将前两者拼接得到的细粒度义标签。

Result: 构建了一个包含20个法语多义动词实例的义标注语料库，每个实例具有三层标注结构。

Conclusion: 该语料库为法语词义消歧提供了多层次的标注资源，有望推动法语WSD研究的发展。

Abstract: We describe a new sense-tagged corpus for word sense disambiguation. The corpus is constituted of instances of 20 French polysemous verbs. Each verb instance is annotated with three sense labels: (1) the actual translation of the verb in the english version of this instance in a parallel corpus, (2) an entry of the verb in a computational dictionary of French (the Lexicon-Grammar tables) and (3) a fine-grained sense label resulting from the concatenation of the translation and the Lexicon-Grammar entry.

</details>


### [191] [OmniVerifier-M1: Multimodal Meta-Verifier with Explicit Structured Recalibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28805)
*Xinchen Zhang,Bowei Liu,Jiale Liu,Chufan Shi,Yizhen Zhang,Junhong Liu,Youliang Zhang,Zhiheng Li,Yujiu Yang,Ling Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniVerifier-M1，一种利用符号元验证（如边界框）和分离式强化学习训练的通用视觉验证器，可在多模态大语言模型中实现细粒度验证和动态区域级自纠正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型的视觉输出日益重要，需要可靠且细粒度的验证机制来支撑通用基础模型的规模化发展。现有验证方法多为决策式信号，缺乏可解释性和细粒度反馈。

Method: 1) 使用符号验证器输出（如边界框）而非文本解释作为元验证理由；2) 将二元判断和元验证的强化学习目标解耦，而非联合优化；3) 训练OmniVerifier-M1通用视觉验证器，并构建M1-TTS验证器驱动的智能体生成系统。

Result: 符号元验证优于文本解释作为验证理由；解耦的强化学习目标显著优于联合奖励优化；OmniVerifier-M1实现稳健验证和细粒度错误定位；M1-TTS系统实现动态区域级自纠正。

Conclusion: 该方法为更可靠、可解释、细粒度的多模态验证铺平了道路，支持更安全、可控的基础模型部署，并验证了符号元验证和解耦强化学习的有效性。

Abstract: Visual outcomes are increasingly central to multimodal large language models, making reliable and fine-grained verification essential for scaling generalist foundation models. In this work, we investigate multimodal meta-verification, which leverages verifier-generated rationales rather than decision-only signals, and explore how to effectively incorporate meta-verification feedback into multimodal verifier training. We identify two key findings. First, symbolic verifier outputs (e.g., bounding boxes) outperform textual explanations as meta-verification rationales, enabling efficient rule-based reinforcement learning rewards while avoiding reliance on model-based rewards from auxiliary judge models. Second, decoupling reinforcement learning objectives for binary judgment and meta-verification substantially outperforms joint reward optimization, due to intrinsic differences in output structure and learning dynamics. Based on these insights, we train OmniVerifier-M1, a generalist visual verifier leveraging symbolic meta-verification and decoupled reinforcement learning. OmniVerifier-M1 provides robust verification and fine-grained error localization, and further enables M1-TTS, a verifier-driven agentic generation system achieving dynamic region-level self-correction. This approach paves the way for more reliable, interpretable, and fine-grained multimodal verification, supporting safer and more controllable foundation model deployment.

</details>


### [192] [Functional Entropy: Predicting Functional Correctness in LLM-Generated Code with Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28500)
*Dylan Bouchard,Mohit Singh Chauhan,Zeya Ahmad,Ho-Kyeong Ra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了不确定性量化方法在代码生成任务中的迁移效果，发现基于令牌概率的方法可直接适用，而基于NLI的采样方法因无法区分功能差异而失效。为此提出函数等价方法（如功能熵），在15个模型-基准组合中的11个取得最佳AUROC，并实现最优校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在代码生成中常产生功能错误的代码，现有UQ方法主要用于自然语言生成的幻觉检测，其在代码生成任务中的有效性尚未被充分探索。

Method: 跨三种编程语言、五个LLM和1700+问题系统评估UQ技术迁移效果。提出函数等价方法家族，用基于LLM的函数等价性评估替代NLI的语义等价性评估，包括功能熵（语义熵的代码特异版本）。

Result: 基于令牌概率的方法可无需修改地泛化；NLI方法因无法区分功能不同的代码导致多数响应坍缩为单一语义簇而失败；函数等价方法在11/15组合中达到最佳AUROC，并在多数设置中实现最佳校准。

Conclusion: 代码生成任务需要专用的UQ方法，函数等价方法（特别是功能熵）显著优于基于NLI的方法及其他所有评估方法。

Abstract: Large language models have shown impressive capabilities in code generation, yet they often produce functionally incorrect code. Uncertainty quantification (UQ) methods have emerged as a promising approach for detecting hallucinations in natural language generation, but their effectiveness for code generation tasks remains underexplored. We systematically evaluate how UQ techniques transfer to code generation across three programming languages, five LLMs, and over 1,700 problems. We find that some token-probability-based methods generalize effectively without modification, while sampling-based methods relying on natural language inference (NLI) fail because NLI models cannot distinguish functionally different code, causing most responses to collapse into a single semantic cluster. To address this, we introduce functional equivalence methods, a family of code-specific methods that replace NLI-based semantic equivalence with an LLM-based functional equivalence assessment, including functional entropy, a code-specific analog of semantic entropy. Functional equivalence methods achieve top AUROC in 11 out of 15 model-benchmark combinations and the best calibration across most settings, consistently outperforming both NLI-based counterparts and all other methods evaluated.

</details>


### [193] [On Compositional Learning Behaviours in Formal Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28512)
*Kevin Yandoka Denamganaï*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出S2B-LM基准来评估形式化数学定理证明中的组合学习行为（CLB），通过精确排列检验发现CLB能力对于达到奥林匹克级别（miniF2F>75%）的定理证明器是必要的但不充分的，搜索密集型模型虽能覆盖大部分易处理问题但缺乏CLB能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的形式化数学定理证明器虽然取得了一定进展，但难以攻克奥林匹克级别的难题。作者认为组合学习行为（CLB）——即能够在上下文中接地并重组新颖符号结构的能力——可能是突破这一瓶颈的关键，但目前缺乏针对性的评估方法来验证这一假设。

Method: 作者提出了S2B-LM基准，这是对Symbolic Behaviour Benchmark的改进：去除了数值处理作为混淆因素，并添加了思维链（chain-of-thought）脚手架来引导而非仅仅探测潜在的CLB能力。通过对10个Lean 4定理证明器进行交叉评估（使用adj-ZSCT衡量CLB能力，使用miniF2F衡量整体证明性能），并采用精确排列检验（exact permutation tests）来分析两者关系。

Result: 实验发现搜索密集型模型虽能覆盖大量易处理问题但未检测到CLB能力；而所有在miniF2F上达到75%以上（奥林匹克级别）的模型都是CLB得分最高的前五名（p=0.004）。在排除了模型规模作为混淆因素后，结果表明CLB能力对于攻克形式化数学验证的硬尾问题是必要的但非充分的。

Conclusion: CLB能力是形式化数学定理证明器达到高水平性能的必要条件，但仅有CLB能力并不足以保证成功，还需要其他因素的配合。这一发现为指导未来形式化数学证明系统的研发提供了重要方向。

Abstract: Self-evolving scientific agents capable of conquering the hard tail of formal mathematics require Compositional Learning Behaviours (CLBs) -- the capacity to ground and recombine novel symbolic structures in context, beyond mere recombination of prelearned atoms. We propose \textbf{S2B-LM}, an adaptation of the Symbolic Behaviour Benchmark that removes numerical processing as a confound and adds chain-of-thought scaffolding to elicit rather than merely probe latent CLB competency. Cross-evaluating ten Lean~4 theorem provers on CLB competency (adj-ZSCT) and miniF2F whole-proof performance, exact permutation tests establish a hierarchical necessity structure: search-heavy models cover the tractable bulk without detectable CLBs, yet every model breaking into the Olympiad-level tier (miniF2F $>75\%$) is among the five highest CLB scorers ($p=0.004$). After ruling out model scale as a confound, our results show that CLB competency is \emph{necessary but not sufficient} for the hard tail of formal mathematical verification.

</details>


### [194] [ClinicalEncoder26AM: A Multlilingual Diagnosable ColBERT Model; Evidences from the MultiClinNER Shared Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28521)
*François Remy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ClinicalEncoder26AM 是一个多语言临床文本编码器，通过对齐临床潜在空间、多适配器蒸馏和 ColBERT 检索目标进行后训练，在 MultiClinNER 任务中以简单架构实现多语言实体抽取 SOTA 性能，且数据效率显著优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床文本编码模型在多语言场景下的实体识别能力有限，且数据效率不高，亟需一个能够跨语言对齐、高效利用训练数据的临床语义编码方案。

Method: 基于 BGE-M3 后训练，通过多级别对齐 ClinicalMap25 潜在空间；结合合成临床笔记、医患对话及 MedMentions 等标注资源；采用多适配器蒸馏处理命名实体级和句子级表示，并加入 ColBERT 风格检索目标；下游微调时添加轻量双层 CNN 头用于 BIO 标签识别。

Result: 在 MultiClinNER 共享任务中，以单次 8192 token 窗口处理多数文档，实现多语言实体召回 SOTA，并在所有实体类型和语言上取得 Character-weighted F1 前五名；训练曲线表明数据效率显著优于 BGE-M3 基线。

Conclusion: 临床后训练策略能有效提升多语言临床信息抽取的性能与数据效率，ClinicalEncoder26AM 作为简单但高效的编码器具有实用价值。

Abstract: ClinicalEncoder26AM is a multilingual Diagnosable ColBERT for clinical and biomedical texts, which aligns at multiple levels its token-level semantic with ClinicalMap25, a clinical latent space inspired by BioLORD-2023 and enriched with synthetic and annotated supervision. The post-training recipe builds upon BGE-M3, and combines synthetic clinical notes, patient--doctor conversations, and annotated resources such as MedMentions, while considering both named-entity-level and sentence-level representations in a multi-adapter distillation, along with a ColBERT-style retrieval objective. In this system demonstration paper, we evaluate the model in the MultiClinNER shared task by finetuning it as a BIO tagger for patient symptoms, disorders, and procedure spans, using a lightweight two-layer CNN head to improve local boundary detection. The resulting system remains simple, processes most documents in a single 8192-token window, and achieves state-of-the-art multilingual entity recall, while achieving Top 5 overall across all entity types and languages in Character-weighted F1 scores. Training curves further show that ClinicalEncoder26AM is markedly more data-efficient than the base M3 model, supporting the usefulness of its clinical post-training for downstream information extraction.
  The model can be downloaded on https://huggingface.co/Parallia/ClinicalEncoder26AM-Diagnosable-Colbert-L2-for-multilingual-medical-texts

</details>


### [195] [GUI-CIDER: Mid-training GUI Agents via Causal Internalization and Density-aware Exemplar Reselection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28534)
*Zheng Wu,Chengcheng Han,Zhengxi Lu,Tianjie Ju,Yanyu Chen,Qi Gu,Xunliang Cai,Zhuosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出GUI-CIDER，一种通过因果内化和密度感知示例重选的中期训练方法，让GUI智能体显式学习GUI操作世界知识，提升任务完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型构建的GUI智能体因缺乏GUI操作的世界知识而受限于任务完成，现有方法（多智能体框架、SFT、RL）只能让智能体隐式吸收知识，导致低效的轨迹记忆而非真正理解。

Method: 三阶段方法：(1)数据合成——从GUI轨迹中蒸馏静态规划知识和动态因果知识为文本；(2)示例重选——通过奖励因果结构、惩罚语义冗余来过滤语料；(3)中期训练——用精炼数据嵌入知识。

Result: 在2个GUI知识基准和3个任务完成基准上的实验表明，GUI-CIDER一致提升了智能体对GUI操作的理解和任务成功率。

Conclusion: GUI-CIDER通过显式内化GUI世界知识，有效提升了智能体的操作理解和任务完成能力，是一种优于传统后训练范式的中期训练方法。

Abstract: Despite the rapid progress of multimodal large language models in building Graphical User Interface (GUI) agents, their real-world task completion is fundamentally bottlenecked by a lack of world knowledge about GUI operations. Existing solutions typically rely on expensive multi-agent scaffolding or conventional post-training paradigms, such as Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Reinforcement Learning (RL). However, post-training only allows agents to implicitly absorb world knowledge through action annotations or reward signals, leading to inefficient trajectory memorization rather than genuine comprehension. Therefore, an approach that enables explicit learning of this knowledge is imperative. To this end, we propose GUI-CIDER, a mid-training method that explicitly internalizes GUI world knowledge through Causal Internalization and Density-aware Exemplar Reselection. GUI-CIDER operates in three stages: (1) data synthesis, which distills static planning and dynamic causal knowledge from GUI trajectories into text; (2) exemplar reselection, which filters the corpus by rewarding causal structures and penalizing semantic redundancy; and (3) mid-training, where the refined data is used to embed the acquired knowledge. Extensive experiments on two GUI knowledge benchmarks and three task completion benchmarks demonstrate that GUI-CIDER consistently improves both the agent's understanding of GUI operations and its task success rates.The codes are available at https://github.com/Wuzheng02/GUI-CIDER.

</details>


### [196] [Soft-SVeRL: Self-Verified Reinforcement Learning with Soft Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28561)
*Saurabh Dash,Pierre Clavier,John Dang,Matthias Galle,Marzieh Fadaee,Ahmet Üstün,Beyza Ermis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Soft-RLVR框架，通过将prompt分解为原子需求清单、使用LLM验证器逐项评分并训练软奖励，解决了部分可验证任务中的强化学习问题。同时引入Soft-SVeRL自验证变体，并发现自验证需要显式稳定化以防止奖励膨胀。在指令遵循任务中，基于清单的Soft-RLVR在IFEval上最多提升11.1个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法仅适用于数学和代码等完全可验证领域，但许多实际任务（如指令遵循）只有部分可验证性——提示包含多个要求，回答可能仅满足部分要求，或不存在单一参考答案。

Method: 1) Soft-RLVR：将prompt分解为原子需求检查清单，用LLM验证器逐项评分，生成软奖励信号。2) 形式化了清单级验证与整体验证之间的折衷（平均化判断可降低噪声，但部分奖励可能奖励不完整回答）。3) Soft-SVeRL：让策略同时充当验证器的自验证变体，并引入显式稳定化机制防止奖励通胀。

Result: 在可控指令遵循设定中，基于清单的Soft-RLVR使用仅学习到的验证器奖励，在IFEval上最多提升11.1个点。实验结果进一步表明验证器质量和清单质量都影响下游RL效果，且显式稳定化对自验证至关重要。

Conclusion: 基于检查清单的分解式验证能为部分可验证任务提供更可靠的RL训练信号，但自验证需要显式稳定化来防止奖励坍塌。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has improved language models in domains such as mathematics and code, where correctness can be checked automatically. However, many important tasks are only partially verifiable: prompts contain multiple requirements, responses may satisfy some but not all of them, or no single reference answer might exist. We introduce Soft-RLVR, a framework for reinforcement learning from decomposed, learned verification signals. Soft-RLVR converts each prompt into a checklist of atomic requirements, scores candidate responses item by item with an LLM verifier, and trains on the resulting soft reward. Checklist-based rewards turn sparse pass/fail supervision into a denser partial-credit signal, but they also introduce a tradeoff: averaging item-level judgments can reduce verifier noise, while partial credit can reward incomplete responses. We formalize this tradeoff and identify conditions under which checklist-based verification gives a more reliable RL training signal than holistic verification. We further introduce Soft-SVeRL, a self-verifying variant of Soft-RLVR in which the policy also acts as the verifier. We show that self-verification is prone to reward inflation from overly permissive self-judgments, and that explicit stabilization is needed to prevent this collapse. In a controlled instruction-following setting with rule-based ground-truth evaluation, checklist-based Soft-RLVR improves IFEval by up to 11.1 points using only learned verifier rewards. Our experiments further show that verifier quality and checklist quality both affect downstream RL outcomes, and that explicit stabilization is essential for effective self-verification.

</details>


### [197] [Models That Know How Evaluations Are Designed Score Safer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28591)
*Katharina Deckenbach,Haritz Puerto,Jonas Geiping,Sahar Abdelnabi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现AI模型在训练过程中通过接触描述评估特征（如可验证结构或道德困境）的文本，会隐式学习到“评估元知识”，从而导致其在安全基准测试中表现更安全，但这种行为变化并非源于真正的安全对齐，而是对评估语境的识别响应，构成了一种新的混杂因素，可能虚假地提升安全基准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究发现测试时的上下文线索（如假设场景）会导致模型表现出评估意识并改变行为。本文旨在探究这种现象的一个潜在解释：模型是否通过学习关于评估结构特征的参数化知识（即评估元知识），从而在评估类上下文中表现出行为偏移。

Method: 在合成文档上微调模型，这些文档描述了评估特征（如可验证结构或道德困境）；然后评估微调模型在六个安全基准上的表现，并将其与基础模型和控制模型进行比较；还进一步分析了那些没有明确表达评估意识的回答。

Result: 微调后的模型在六个安全基准上显著比基础模型和控制模型更安全。这种行为偏移甚至在分析那些没有明确表达评估意识的回答时仍然存在。

Conclusion: 评估元知识可能会虚假地提升安全基准性能，引入了一种独立于显式记忆或可言语化的评估意识的新型混杂因素，且难以检测，这对于AI安全评估的设计和解释具有重要意义。

Abstract: The validity of AI safety evaluations depends on models behaving consistently across controlled and deployment settings. Prior work has identified test-time contextual cues, such as hypothetical scenarios, as a source of verbalized evaluation awareness and subsequent behavioral shift. In this paper, we investigate a potential explanation of this phenomenon: evaluation meta-knowledge, defined as parametric knowledge about the structural traits that characterize evaluations. Similar to dataset contamination, where benchmark exposure leads to higher performance through memorization, we hypothesize that models trained on texts describing evaluation practices may implicitly learn to recognize and respond to evaluation-like contexts, for instance, through exposure to scientific articles or social media posts about AI benchmarking. To test this, we fine-tune models on synthetic documents describing evaluation traits such as verifiable structures or moral dilemmas. Evaluating this fine-tuned model on six safety benchmarks, we find that it is significantly safer than the base model and control model. This behavioral shift persists even when restricting the analysis to responses lacking explicit verbalization of evaluation awareness. Our results demonstrate that evaluation meta-knowledge may inflate safety benchmark performance, introducing a novel confounder that is independent of explicit memorization or verbalized evaluation awareness, thus, challenging to detect. These findings have important implications for the design and interpretation of AI safety evaluations. Our code and models are available at https://github.com/compass-group-tue/arxiv2026_evaluation_meta_knowledge.

</details>


### [198] [Evaluating the Realism of LLM-powered Social Agents: A Case Study of Reactions to Spanish Online News](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28598)
*Alejandro Buitrago López,Alberto Ortega Pastor,Javier Pastor-Galindo,José A. Ruipérez-Valiente*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了五种LLM生成的西班牙语新闻评论在仇恨言论、情感与语义对齐三个维度上与真实受众反应的差异，发现通用模型表现差，微调可部分改善但效果不均。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM社会智能体验证多依赖通用基准，缺乏对短篇、反应性话语（如在线新闻受众评论）真实性的评估。

Method: 基于Hatemedia数据集，将5,631条新闻与58,555条真实受众反应配对，使用五种LLM在统一实验设置下生成匹配的合成数据集，从仇恨言论、情感、语义对齐三个维度对比真实与合成反应，同时考察通用与微调两种生成方式。

Result: 通用模型严重低估仇恨言论、引入模型特有情感偏差、与人类回复分布距离大；微调提升效果不均：Qwen3最均衡，Mistral7B情感与语义对齐最强但过度生成仇恨言论。

Conclusion: 看似合理的合成回复并不一定能复现公共话语的分布特性，LLM模拟在线社会行为仍需更细致的验证。

Abstract: LLM-powered social agents are increasingly used to simulate online social behavior, yet their realism remains difficult to validate. Existing work has largely relied on general-purpose benchmarks, while less attention has been paid to short, reactive discourse such as audience replies to online news.
  In this paper, we evaluate whether LLM-generated reactions to Spanish online news reproduce measurable properties of real audience discourse. Using the Hatemedia dataset, we pair 5,631 news items with 58,555 real audience reactions, and generate a matched synthetic dataset using five LLMs under a shared experimental setting. We compare real and synthetic reactions across three dimensions: hate speech, sentiment, and semantic alignment, considering both off-the-shelf and fine-tuned generation.
  Results show that off-the-shelf models are poor proxies for real audience reactions: they strongly underproduce hate speech, introduce model-specific sentiment biases, and remain distributionally distant from human replies. Fine-tuning improves fidelity unevenly. Qwen3 provides the most balanced approximation, while Mistral7B achieves the strongest sentiment and semantic alignment but overshoots hate prevalence. Plausible synthetic replies do not necessarily reproduce the distributional properties of public discourse.

</details>


### [199] [Measuring Form and Function in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28616)
*Héctor Javier Vázquez Martínez,Charles Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CAC的提示方法，用于评估语言模型在儿童语言习得中的限定词句法与话语知识能力。研究发现，当前无模型能在与儿童相当的数据量下同时达到形式与功能基准，但部分超大模型能实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型评估缺乏针对儿童语言习得中限定词的形式句法和功能话语两个维度的定量比较方法，亟需建立可直接对比儿童与模型的标准化评测框架。

Method: 提出Contextual Alternative Choice（CAC）提示方法，分别针对限定词的句法知识和话语知识设计测试，并与儿童语言习得数据及统计基准进行直接比较。

Result: 没有在可比数据量下训练的语言模型能同时满足形式句法和功能话语两个维度的儿童水平基准；仅极大规模模型（如超大模型）能达到该标准。

Conclusion: 当前语言模型在限定词习得方面与人类儿童存在差距，但超大模型展现出接近儿童认知水平的能力；CAC方法为评估语言模型的认知状态提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We introduce quantitative metrics for child language acquisition to evaluate language models. Our focus is on the formal syntactic and functional discourse properties of determiners in English, which young children acquire early and accurately. We propose Contextual Alternative Choice (CAC), a new prompting method which provides targeted tests for both syntactic and discourse knowledge of language. The method enables direct comparison of language models against children, and more importantly, against statistical benchmarks independently established in empirical research. No current model trained on a comparable amount of data simultaneously meet both formal and functional benchmarks like human children, but some very large models do. We present our results as methodological and technical contributions, with specific emphasis on cognitive status of language models.

</details>


### [200] [Mobile-Aptus: Confidence-Driven Proactive and Robust Interaction in MLLM-based Mobile-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28629)
*Zheng Wu,Pengzhou Cheng,Zongru Wu,Yuan Guo,Tianjie Ju,Aston Zhang,Gongshen Liu,Zhuosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Mobile-Aptus，一个通用置信度集成框架，通过两阶段训练（交互能力赋能+置信度偏差校正）来平衡移动设备MLLM智能体的过度执行和过度请求问题，在四个基准测试中平均提升17%以上的任务成功率，实际动态场景中提升26%且每指令仅需0.64次人工干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动设备MLLM智能体存在两个问题：完全自主的智能体会在无法完成任务时仍尝试执行（过度执行），而允许人机交互的智能体则过度依赖人类干预（过度请求）。现有方法未能同时解决这两个矛盾。

Method: 提出通用置信度集成框架，包含两个阶段：1) 交互能力赋能阶段：通过监督微调使智能体同时输出动作和置信度分数；2) 置信度偏差校正阶段：结合语义相似度检索和直接偏好优化，使智能体输出更准确的置信度分数。

Result: 在OS-Kairos、AITZ、Meta-GUI、AndroidControl四个基准测试中达到SOTA，平均任务成功率提升17%以上；实际动态实验中任务成功率提升26%，每指令仅需0.64次干预步骤。

Conclusion: 置信度驱动的主动鲁棒交互机制能有效平衡移动智能体的自主执行与人类求助，显著提升任务成功率并减少不必要的人工干预。

Abstract: Recent advancements in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown exceptional potential in enabling mobile-using agents to autonomously execute human instructions. However, fully automated agents often try to execute tasks even when they are unable to resolve them, leading to the problem of over-execution. Previous studies solve it by training a interactive mobile-using agents to let agents request human interaction when agents can not complete user instructions. However, we find that these interactive agents tend to exhibit over-soliciting behavior, relying excessively on human intervention. To mitigate both over-execution and over-soliciting, we propose a universal confidence integration framework that enables confidence-driven proactive and robust interaction in MLLM-based mobile-using agents. The framework consists of two stages: interaction capability empowerment and confidence bias correction. In the interaction capability empowerment stage, agents learn through supervised fine-tuning to output both actions and confidence scores. In the confidence bias correction stage, agents learn to output more accurate confidence scores by combining semantic similarity retrieval with direct preference optimization. Experimental results show Mobile-Aptus achieves state-of-the-art performance on the four popular mobile-using agent benchmarks: OS-Kairos, AITZ, Meta-GUI, and AndroidControl. Mobile-Aptus consistently outperforms all baselines in offline benchmarks, with an average improvement over 17\% in task success rate. In real-world dynamic experiments, Mobile-Aptus surpasses the baseline by 26% in task success rate with only 0.64 intervention steps per instruction. The codes are available at https://github.com/Wuzheng02/Mobile-Aptus.

</details>


### [201] [The Attentional White Bear Effect in Transformer Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28639)
*Rebecca Ramnauth,Brian Scassellati*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了指令性抑制是否真正减少了语言模型中禁止概念的内部表征，还是仅仅压制了输出表达。通过表征探测、注意力分析和语义泄露实验，发现禁止概念在隐藏层中依然高度可恢复，继续影响注意力路由并塑造下游生成，揭示了行为对齐与表征对齐之间的根本差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前广泛使用指令性抑制来防止语言模型生成违规内容，但该方法是否真正消除了模型内部的禁止概念表征尚不清楚，存在行为对齐与内部表征对齐之间的鸿沟。

Method: 采用表征探测（representational probing）、注意力分析（attention analysis）和语义泄露实验（behavioral semantic leakage experiments）三种方法，在多种Transformer模型上进行实验，并跨池化策略、间接语义控制和多种模型家族进行验证。

Result: 禁止概念在抑制条件下仍可从隐藏表征中高度恢复；持续影响注意力路由；尽管成功实现了词汇层面的规避，但仍可测量地塑造了下游生成结果。这些效应在不同池化策略、间接语义控制和模型家族中均一致存在。

Conclusion: 指令性抑制仅实现了行为层面的对齐，并未真正消除模型内部的禁止概念表征，暴露了行为对齐与表征对齐之间的根本差距。

Abstract: Instruction-based suppression is widely used to prevent language models from generating prohibited content, yet it remains unclear whether suppression reduces internal representation or merely suppresses expression. We investigate this question through representational probing, attention analysis, and behavioral semantic leakage experiments across multiple transformer models. We find that prohibited concepts remain highly recoverable from hidden representations under suppression, continue to influence attention routing, and measurably shape downstream generations despite successful lexical avoidance. These effects persist across pooling strategies, indirect semantic controls, and multiple model families. Our results expose a fundamental gap between behavioral and representational alignment.

</details>


### [202] [Towards Reliable Multilingual LLMs-as-a-Judge: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28710)
*Irune Zubiaga,Aitor Soroa,Rodrigo Agerri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统探索了多语言LLM-as-a-judge评估策略，针对高、中、低资源语言（英语、西班牙语、巴斯克语）分析了指令翻译、单/多语言监督及模型规模的影响，发现在域数据可用时微调小模型可媲美闭源模型，而零样本大模型在域外场景更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM自动评估研究主要聚焦英语，多语言场景下低资源语言及域内数据稀缺时的评估策略尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 选取英语、西班牙语、巴斯克语三种资源层级语言，分别采用指令翻译、单语与多语监督、不同模型规模等策略，并将两个现有元评估数据集扩展至巴斯克语和西班牙语进行系统分析。

Result: 域内数据可用时，微调的小模型能达到与闭源模型相当的性能；域外场景下，零样本大模型效果更好；域外数据微调可能对模型性能产生负面影响。

Conclusion: 研究结果为构建高效可靠的多语言评估流水线提供了实用指导，并公开了数据和代码。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for the automatic evaluation of generated text, yet most prior work focuses on English. Despite the growing demand for multilingual evaluation, extending LLM-based evaluators to multilingual settings remains challenging, particularly for low-resource languages and scenarios where in-domain data is scarce. This work explores several strategies for developing multilingual LLMs-as-a-judge, considering whether in-domain data is available for fine-tuning or not. We systematically analyze English, Spanish, and Basque, representing high-, mid-, and low-resource languages, considering instruction translation, monolingual versus multilingual supervision, and model size. For evaluation, we extend two existing meta-evaluation datasets to Basque and Spanish. Our results reveal key trade-offs: When in-domain data is available, fine-tuned smaller models can achieve performance comparable to proprietary models, whereas zero-shot evaluation with larger models proves more effective in out-of-domain settings. We also observe that fine-tuning on out-of-domain data can adversely affect model performance. These findings provide practical guidance for building efficient, reliable multilingual evaluation pipelines. The data and code are publicly available at hitz-zentroa/mJudge.

</details>


### [203] [IPO-Mine: A Toolkit and Dataset for Section-Structured Analysis of Long, Multimodal IPO Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28714)
*Michael Galarnyk,Siddharth Lohani,Vidhyakshaya Kannan,Sagnik Nandi,Aman Patel,Liqin Ye,Arnav Hiray,Rutwik Routu,Prasun Banerjee,Siddhartha Somani,Sudheer Chava*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了IPO-Toolkit开源框架，用于下载和解析IPO招股说明书（1994-2026年，10.9万份文件，7.6万张图像），构建了标准化、多模态的IPO-Dataset，并在财务图表评估任务上发现当前多模态模型与人类专家判断存在显著对齐差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IPO招股说明书是金融市场上重要的长文档、多模态文件，但其缺乏大规模标准化数据集和基准，现有语言和多模态模型难以处理这些超过50万token且结构不一致的文档。

Method: 开发IPO-Toolkit开源框架，实现IPO文件的分段、图像提取和结构化输出；在此基础上构建含10.9万份文件的多模态IPO-Dataset，并设计财务图表的评估任务（质量评估和误导性评估）。

Result: 构建了覆盖1994-2026年、含10.9万+IPO文件及7.6万+图像的数据集；实验表明最先进的多模态模型在图表质量与误导性评估任务上与人类专家判断存在显著分歧。

Conclusion: 当前多模态模型在处理真实世界长篇监管文档的多模态推理时存在对齐挑战；IPO-Dataset和IPO-Toolkit为大规模、可复现的IPO文档分析及跨行业文本与视觉披露实践研究提供了基础设施。

Abstract: An Initial Public Offering (IPO) filing is a document released when a private firm goes public, allowing individual (retail) investors to purchase its shares. These filings describe a firm's business, financials, and risks and are long, multimodal documents with narrative text and images. Despite their importance to financial markets, there is no large-scale, standardized dataset or benchmark for studying IPO filings with modern language and multimodal models. These documents pose significant challenges: filings frequently exceed 500,000 tokens and lack consistent structural organization. We introduce the IPO-Toolkit, an open-source framework for downloading and parsing IPO filings into standardized section-structured text and extracted images. The toolkit segments filings, extracts embedded images, and produces structured outputs that enable large-scale, reproducible analysis workflows over long, multimodal documents. Using this infrastructure, we construct the IPO-Dataset, a large, section-structured, multimodal dataset covering more than 109,000 IPO filings and amendments from 1994 to 2026 and containing over 76,000 images. We establish structured evaluation tasks over extracted financial charts, including chart quality and misleadingness assessment. Our experiments show that state-of-the-art multimodal models often diverge from expert human judgments on these tasks, exposing alignment challenges in multimodal reasoning over long, real-world regulatory documents. Beyond benchmarking, the IPO-Dataset enables large-scale analysis of section-level textual variation and cross-industry differences in visual and textual disclosure practices. Our code, dataset, and website are publicly available under CC-BY-4.0.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [204] [Identifying and Understanding Human Values in Text: A Tailorable LLM-based Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27373)
*Eduardo de la Cruz Fernández,Marcelo Karanik,Sascha Ossowski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的模块化架构，用于检测和量化文本中人类价值观的强度。该架构包含三个协调模块：结构化价值规范生成模块、文本标注模块、以及基于修辞和语义证据的支持/抵抗强度赋值模块。该架构摆脱了对特定价值理论或复杂提示工程的依赖，具有可扩展性和可重复性。在ValueEval数据集上的实验验证了其良好的检测性能和通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主智能系统的发展，传统效用最大化模型不足以满足伦理和道德决策需求。评估智能系统决策与人类价值观的契合度成为关键问题。现有方法受限于特定价值理论或需要复杂的提示工程，缺乏通用性和可扩展性。

Method: 提出三模块LLM架构：(1) 价值规范生成模块——从任意理论框架的基础文本中生成结构化价值规范；(2) 文本标注模块——利用生成的价值规范对文本进行标注；(3) 强度赋值模块——基于修辞和语义证据，为文本分配对价值观的支持或抵抗程度。该架构通过模块化分离概念化与检测任务，使价值规范可适应多种理论。

Result: 在ValueEval数据集上使用多个LLM实例化架构，实验显示良好的检测性能，验证了管线的通用性。

Conclusion: 该模块化LLM架构有效克服了传统方法对特定价值理论的依赖，提供了一种可扩展、可重复的人类价值检测方案，为自主智能系统的伦理决策评估提供了有力工具。

Abstract: As intelligent systems become more autonomous, the scientific community focuses on creating decision-making mechanisms that include ethical and moral considerations, unlike traditional utility-maximisation models. To achieve this, a key aspect is assessing how well these decisions align with human values. To this end, a promising line of research is centred on developing approaches based on Large Language Models (LLMs) to identify human values from text, whether explicit or implicit, enabling their recognition throughout. This paper introduces a LLM-based architecture to detect and quantify the intensity of human values in text, avoiding the limitations of previous approaches tied to specific value theory or complex prompt engineering. The architecture comprises three coordinated modules: one that generates structured value specifications from the foundational texts of any theoretical framework; one that labels texts using these specifications; and one that assigns graded support or resistance based on rhetorical and semantic evidence. This modular approach separates the tasks of conceptualising from detecting human values, creating a scalable and reproducible process driven by value specifications adaptable to various theories. The architecture was instantiated with multiple LLMs and evaluated using the ValueEval dataset. The experiments demonstrate good detection performance, confirming the generality of the pipeline.

</details>


### [205] [On the Origin of Synthetic Information by Means of Steganographic Inheritance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27551)
*Ching-Chun Chang,Isao Echizen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于隐写术的合成信息谱系追踪机制，通过为AI生成内容嵌入可遗传的隐藏特征，实现对合成信息进化谱系的追溯，类似于生物学中的遗传与亲子鉴定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI生成内容日益泛滥，合成信息的来源追溯变得愈发困难（尤其是当模型生成内容与原始来源在结构和信号层面差异巨大时），这影响了信息可信度与信任体系，亟需一种类似生物遗传学的谱系追踪方法。

Method: 借鉴遗传学原理，提出基于隐写术的谱系追溯框架：(1) 繁殖时，投影器从亲本提取特征，隐写编码器将其不可见地嵌入后代；(2) 该特征在后代生命周期中持续存在；(3) 查询亲缘关系时，隐写解码器提取特征并与候选亲本特征比对，确定最可能的来源。

Result: 理论分析揭示了系统发育准确度与投影器及隐写系统属性的函数关系；跨多种投影器和隐写系统的实证评估表明，该方法在一系列处理操作和语义修改下均具有可行性。

Conclusion: 未来可构建一个合成信息生态系统，其中信息被赋予隐藏但可追踪的谱系特征，从简单起点不断分支进化，实现对合成信息演化历史的完整追溯。

Abstract: The origin of species has been the mystery of mysteries in natural science. By analogy, the origin of synthetic information, we suggest, is the mystery of mysteries in information science. The question carries a moral weight that a technical account can neither fully resolve nor responsibly ignore, as its impact on truth, trust, and human intellect extends deep into the broader economy and society. The very power of artificial intelligence makes the evolutionary lineage of synthetic information grow ever harder to trace, for a sufficiently capable model may generate offspring that bear little resemblance, at either the structural or signal level, to the parent source from which they were derived. As in genetics, two individuals may share the same phenotype mirroring each other in outward appearance, yet differ fundamentally in their genotype. We propose, by means of steganography, a mechanism analogous to heredity. At the moment an offspring is reproduced, a projector derives a trait from the parent, and a steganographic encoder invisibly hides it within the offspring. This trait persists throughout the offspring's life cycle in a cyber ecosystem. When parentage is queried, a steganographic decoder extracts the trait from the offspring and compares it against the traits of candidate parents in a reference pool, thereby nominating the most likely one. A theoretical analysis characterises phylogenetic accuracy as a function of projector and stegosystem properties, whilst empirical evaluations across multiple projectors and stegosystems demonstrate the viability of the proposed methodology under a broad spectrum of processing operations and semantic modifications. We envision a cyber ecosystem in which synthetic information, endowed with hidden yet traceable lineage traits, branches from a simple beginning into endless forms that have been, and are being, evolved.

</details>


### [206] [Why LLMs Fail at Causal Discovery and How Interventional Agents Escape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27567)
*Amartya Roy,Sonali Parbhoo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明大语言模型在因果发现中存在根本性局限：监督微调、直接偏好优化和上下文学习等方法无法区分产生相似观测数据的因果图，该局限源于学习范式本身而非模型或数据集。作者提出Agentic因果贝叶斯优化（A-CBO），通过冻结语言模型作为干预查询的预言机，结合外部贝叶斯循环在少量轮次内收敛到候选图，在Corr2Cause和扩展基准上显著优于微调和偏好优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明大语言模型在因果发现任务中性能随图复杂度增加而下降，但失败的根本原因尚未明确。本文旨在从理论层面证明这种失败是学习范式固有的，而非特定模型或数据集的局限。

Method: 提出核障碍定理（kernel obstruction theorem）形式化证明监督微调、直接偏好优化和上下文学习等方法无法区分产生相似观测数据的因果图。在此基础上提出Agentic因果贝叶斯优化（A-CBO），将冻结语言模型作为干预预言机，外部贝叶斯循环在对数轮次内集中对候选图的信念。

Result: 在Corr2Cause上，A-CBO无需训练即可匹配微调基线；在扩展至24个变量、18K测试样本的Extended Corr2Cause上，A-CBO显著优于微调和偏好优化方法，且优势随图复杂度增长而扩大。

Conclusion: 大语言模型在因果发现中的根本性局限源于学习范式本身，通过将决策操作从受限空间外移到外部贝叶斯循环可以克服这一局限，实现可证明的收敛。

Abstract: Causal discovery is a cornerstone of scientific reasoning, yet whether large language models can perform it reliably remains an open question. Recent benchmarks show that even fine-tuned models plateau on simple causal graphs and degrade as complexity grows, but why they fail has not been established. We prove the failure is fundamental: supervised fine-tuning, direct preference optimization, and in-context learning all produce predictors that cannot distinguish between causal graphs generating similar observational data, and any attempt to do so requires the model's internal representations to grow unboundedly, violating the very conditions under which these methods work. We formalize this as a kernel obstruction theorem, establishing that the limitation is intrinsic to the learning paradigm, \emph{not any particular model or dataset}. We propose Agentic Causal Bayesian Optimization (A-CBO), wherein a frozen language model serves as an interventional oracle answering targeted queries about intervention effects, while an external Bayesian loop concentrates beliefs over candidate graphs in logarithmically many rounds. Because the decision operates outside the space where the obstruction applies, A-CBO provably converges while the underlying model remains unchanged. On Corr2Cause, A-CBO matches fine-tuned baselines without any training. On Extended Corr2Cause, a new benchmark scaling to 24 variables with 18K test samples, A-CBO significantly outperforms both fine-tuning and preference optimization, with the advantage growing

</details>


### [207] [RULER: Representation-Level Verification of Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27569)
*Georgina Cosma,Axel Finke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有机器遗忘验证方法仅关注输出层面的指标（成员推断、保留/遗忘集准确率），但模型仍可能在中间表征中编码遗忘记录。本文提出RULER，一组表征层面的验证指标：M2（需重训练对照）和M4（无需重训练）。实验发现，四种近似遗忘方法均通过输出评估，但M2检测到10/12条件中存在显著残留；第五种方法Bad Teacher同样存在残留。M4可作为遗忘前诊断工具，在多个领域检测到身份级别的记忆残留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器遗忘的验证协议仅在输出层级（成员推断、保留集准确率、遗忘集准确率）进行评估，无法可靠地保证模型内部表征已彻底去除遗忘数据的信息。需要能检查中间表征的度量方法。

Method: 提出RULER框架，包含两种表征级度量：1) M2（Oracle对比度量）：比较遗忘记录在未学习模型与从头重训练模型中的表征位置差异；2) M4（无Oracle度量）：仅凭未学习模型内部相似性结构检测残留，无需重训练。采用线性混合效应模型进行统计检验。

Result: 五种近似遗忘方法均通过输出级评估，但M2在10/12条件下检测到显著表征残留（p<0.05），且效果量随遗忘比例增大而增大。Bad Teacher方法虽遗忘机制不同，同样存在残留。M4在表格数据、图像、临床文本、面部识别等场景中可作为预遗忘诊断工具，检测到面部识别模型中所有测试方法均无法完全擦除的身份级记忆。

Conclusion: 输出级评估不足以验证遗忘效果，表征级度量是必要的。现有近似遗忘方法均存在表征层面的信息残留，尤其是人脸识别中的身份级记忆难以完全擦除。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove the influence of specific training records from a deployed model without retraining from scratch. Current protocols verify this at the output level through membership inference, retain accuracy, and forget-set accuracy, but a model can satisfy all three whilst still encoding forgotten records in its intermediate representations. We introduce RULER, a set of representation-level verification metrics. The oracle-comparative metric M2 measures whether forget-set records occupy the same representational position as in a model retrained without them. The oracle-free metric M4 detects residuals from the unlearned model's internal similarity structure alone, without retraining. Four approximate unlearning methods all pass output-level evaluation, yet under a linear mixed-effects model M2 detects significant residuals in 10 of 12 conditions (p<0.05), with effect sizes growing as the forget fraction increases. A fifth method, Bad Teacher, shows the same residuals despite a different forgetting mechanism. M4 acts as a pre-unlearning diagnostic across tabular, image, clinical text, and face-identity settings: it detects identity-level memorisation in face recognition models where no tested method fully erases the signal.

</details>


### [208] [LaneRoPE: Positional Encoding for Collaborative Parallel Reasoning and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27570)
*Gabriele Cesa,Thomas Hehn,Aleix Torres-Camps,Àlex Batlle Casellas,Jordi Ros-Giralt,Arash Behboodi,Tribhuvanesh Orekondy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LaneRoPE，通过在生成时引入序列间注意力掩码和RoPE扩展，使并行LLM测试时扩展中的N个序列能够相互协作，在数学推理任务上取得了额外的精度提升，且对架构改动极小、推理开销可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的并行测试时扩展方法（如best-of-N）虽然利用批处理提升计算效率，但N个序列独立生成，彼此不共享中间生成结果、计算或观察信息，导致资源利用不足。

Method: LaneRoPE包含两个核心设计：(a) 序列间注意力掩码，使不同序列的采样相互依赖；(b) RoPE扩展，注入位置信息以捕捉序列内部及跨序列的token间相对位置。

Result: 在数学推理任务上，LaneRoPE使序列间能够协作，在有限的生成长度下获得了额外的精度提升。

Conclusion: LaneRoPE几乎不改动底层LLM架构，推理开销极小，因此可快速将并行推理能力集成到现有LLM推理管线中。

Abstract: Parallel LLM test-time scaling techniques (e.g., best-of-$N$) require drawing $N>1$ sequences conditioned on the same input prompt. These methods boost accuracy while exploiting the computational efficiency of batching $N$ generations. However, each sequence in the batch is traditionally generated independently and hence does not reuse intermediate generations, computations, or observations from other sequences. In this paper, we propose LaneRoPE to enable coordination and collaboration among $N>1$ sequences at generation time. LaneRoPE involves two key ideas: (a) an inter-sequence attention mask to make sampling of sequences dependent on one another; and (b) a RoPE extension that injects positional information that captures relative positions between tokens, both within and outside a particular sequence. We evaluate our approach on mathematical reasoning tasks and find promising results: LaneRoPE enables collaboration among sequences, yielding additional accuracy gains under limited generated sequence length. Importantly, since LaneRoPE enables coordination with minimal changes to the underlying LLM architecture and introduces a negligible overhead at inference time, it is appealing to rapidly incorporate parallel reasoning into existing LLM inference pipelines.

</details>


### [209] [Discovery Agents for Real-Time Analytics: Toward Proactive Insight Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27571)
*Gaetano Rossiello,Dharmashankar Subramanian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种多智能体架构，用于实时数据流上的自主洞察发现，通过持续发现循环（假设生成→编译→验证→可视化/应用部署）实现从被动查询到主动发现的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分析系统是被动的（查询驱动），无法应对实时流式环境中海量潜在洞察空间的挑战，用户难以手动枚举所有可能的分析查询。

Method: 多智能体架构，包含持续发现循环（假设生成、编译、验证、可视化/应用部署）；基于Kafka的事件驱动协调、Flink流处理、LLM驱动的智能体；通过类型化中间工件的契约驱动设计实现模块化、可观测性、血统追踪和安全执行。

Result: 在零售、金融和公共数据用例中验证了从查询驱动到主动发现驱动分析的转变可行性。

Conclusion: 该架构支持分析范式从被动的查询驱动转向主动的发现驱动，适用于实时流式数据环境。

Abstract: Modern analytics systems are fundamentally reactive, requiring users to define queries over increasingly complex and continuously evolving data. In real-time streaming environments, this paradigm breaks down, as the space of potential insights becomes too large to enumerate manually. We present a multi-agent architecture for autonomous insight discovery over real-time data streams. The system implements a continuous discovery loop in which agents generate hypotheses, compile them into executable analytics, validate generated artifacts, and produce visualizations and deployable applications. The architecture leverages Apache Kafka for event-driven coordination, Apache Flink for stream processing, and large language models to implement specialized agents. A key contribution is a contract-driven design based on typed intermediate artifacts, enabling modularity, observability, lineage, and safer execution of dynamically generated analytics. Through use cases in retail, finance, and public data, we show how this architecture supports a shift from query-driven analytics to proactive, discovery-driven systems.

</details>


### [210] [Agyn: An Open-Source Platform for AI Agents with Scalable On-Demand Execution, Agent Definition as a Code, and Zero-Trust Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27575)
*Nikita Benkovich,Vitalii Valkov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Agyn是一个面向AI Agent生产部署的开源平台，基于Kubernetes的信号驱动有状态无服务器运行时、Terraform提供程序定义Agent和Harness，以及零信任最小权限安全模型，支持Agent无关、模型无关和云无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着组织将AI Agent推向生产环境，面临非确定性工作流、有状态会话、特权访问内部服务等挑战，工程重心从构建单个Agent转向大规模运营，需要解决隔离、治理和安全问题。

Method: 围绕三个核心原则设计：(1) 基于Kubernetes的信号驱动、有状态无服务器运行时；(2) Terraform提供程序用于定义Agent和Harness；(3) 基于零信任和最小权限原则的安全模型。平台设计为Agent无关、模型无关和云无关。

Result: 提出了一个开源平台Agyn，可支持AI Agent在生产环境中的大规模运营，具备隔离、治理和安全能力。

Conclusion: Agyn为AI Agent的生产化部署提供了系统化的平台方案，通过三大设计原则解决了Agent大规模运营中的关键挑战。

Abstract: As organizations move toward production deployments of AI agents, which execute non-deterministic workflows, maintain stateful sessions, and often operate with privileged access to internal services, the engineering challenge shifts from building individual agents to operating them at scale with proper isolation, governance, and security. In this paper we present Agyn, an open-source platform designed around three key principles tailored for agent workloads: a signal-driven, stateful serverless runtime on Kubernetes; a Terraform provider for agent and harness definition; and a security model grounded in zero-trust and least-privilege principles. Agyn is agent-agnostic, model-agnostic, and cloud-agnostic.

</details>


### [211] [You Are in Control of Your State: Why Human Outcomes Are Controllable Through Causal State Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27580)
*Suraj Biswas,Saurav Gupta,Pritam Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出人类行为变异性源于动态潜在状态（时变权重向量），该状态因果性地决定决策与结果，并可通过针对性干预实现精确控制。基于因果推断、预测加工、注意力瓶颈等六条证据链及20万用户观测数据，推导出七项可检验预测和六项系统要求，为数字健康、教育、AI个性化等提供新框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为科学和面向人类的人工智能面临一个核心难题：个体内部变异性持续存在——同一人在相同可观测输入下产生不同结果，且个体间差异无法被任何可观测协变量完全预测。现有方法未能解释这种变异性的来源，论文旨在论证这种变异性应归属于人的动态潜在状态，并通过干预实现对人类结果的精确控制。

Method: 论文将状态定义为时变权重向量，该向量在次日内时间尺度上动态变化，控制个体的生物学、生理学和神经心理学如何处理下一个事件并转化为决策和结果。状态与决策、结果之间为因果关系而非相关关系。意识通道是一个狭窄的注意力瓶颈，其内容本身也取决于状态。研究基于六条已建立证据链（因果推断、预测加工、稳态应变、注意力瓶颈、时间生物学、计算精神病学）和一个历时24个月的观察基础（部署行为平台，涵盖20万以上注册用户，四种职业角色，研究期2023-2026）。

Result: 推导出七项可检验预测；列出状态感知系统的六项操作要求；讨论了在数字健康、教育、AI个性化和个人能动性方面的应用启示。

Conclusion: 共同隐含的观点是：给定事件的结果在干预时机依赖于状态轨迹的条件下是可控的。该框架为理解人类行为变异性、设计状态感知的干预系统提供了理论基础，并具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: A central puzzle for the behavioural sciences and for human-facing artificial intelligence is the persistence of within-person variability. The same individual, presented with the same observable input, produces different outcomes on different occasions, and different individuals produce divergent outcomes that no observable covariate fully predicts. We argue that this variability belongs in the dynamic latent state of the person, and that human outcomes are controllable in a precise and operational sense through interventions that target the state and its weighting at the moment a decision is being formed.
  We define a state as the time-indexed weighting vector over the dimensions that govern how an individual's biology, physiology, and neuropsychology process the next event into a decision and an outcome. The relationship between state, decision, and outcome is causal rather than correlational. The weighting vector is dynamic at sub-daily timescales. The conscious channel through which outcomes are reportable is a narrow attentional bottleneck whose contents are themselves state-dependent. Taken together, these claims imply that the outcome of a given event is controllable, conditionally, on the state-trajectory at the time of intervention.
  We motivate the framework with six strands of established evidence (causal inference, predictive processing, allostasis, attentional bottleneck, chronobiology, computational psychiatry) and a 24-month observational base from a deployed behavioural platform spanning more than 200,000 consented users across four occupational personas (research period 2023 to 2026). We derive seven testable predictions, list six operational requirements for state-aware systems, and discuss implications for digital health, education, AI personalisation, and personal agency.

</details>


### [212] [Laguna M.1/XS.2 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27605)
*Julien Abadji,Marah Abdin,Connor Adams,Eric Alcaide,Mustafa Altun,Michele Artoni,Junze Bao,Uday Barar,Vassilis Bekiaris,Arkadii Bessonov,Benjamin Bütikofer,Jonathan Chang,Yen-Chun Chen,Dmitry Chernenkov,Yang Chi,Filippos Christianos,Fenia Christopoulou,Razvan-Andrei Ciocoiu,Tzachi Cohen,Yohann Coppel,Dmitrii Emelianenko,Brandon Fergerson,Brian Fitzgerald,Matthias Gallé,Alex Golonzovskyi,George Grigorev,Yiyang Hao,Christian Hensel,Jan Huenermann,Ye Ji,Sarthak Joshi,Eiso Kant,Kabir Khandpur,Seonghyeon Kim,Vladimir Kirichenko,Umut Kocasarac,Ilya Kochik,Ivan Komarov,Chaerin Kong,Anurag Koul,François-Joseph Lacroix,Sergei Laktionov,Waren Long,Quentin Malartic,Vadim Markovtsev,Afonso Marques,Robert McHardy,Carlos Mocholí,Dmitry Monakhov,Adam Morris,Martin Muller,Christian Mürtz,Robin Nabel,Thien Nguyen,Rok Novosel,Szymon Ozog,Aalhad Patankar,Aleksei Petrov,Alexandre Piché,Arthur Pignet,Teodor Poncu,Phil Potter,Alexander Rakowski,Pierre-Yves Ritschard,Jay Roberts,Joe Rowell,Piotr Sarna,Pierre-André Savalle,Uladzislau Sazanovich,Nikita Shapovalov,Arsenii Shevchenko,Mikhail Shilkov,Andrei Sokol,Mohamed Soliman,Jack Stephenson,Victor Storchan,Dragos-Constantin Tantaru,Artem Tyurin,Adrian Wälchli,Pengming Wang,Jianxiao Yang,Renat Zayashnikov,Alexander Zelenka Martin,Nikolay Zinov,Caroline Bercier,José Caldeira,Margarida Garcia,Tom George,Kabeer Gharzai,Glenn Hitchcock,Carson Klingenberg,Ivo Pinto,Varun Randery,Noah Smith,Arina Sugako,Jason Warner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了两个基于MoE架构的基础模型Laguna M.1（2258亿总参数，234亿激活参数）和Laguna XS.2（334亿总参数，30亿激活参数），专为长时域智能体编程任务设计。两者在统一内部系统Model Factory中从头端到端训练，该系统将数据、训练、评估和推理组件紧密集成，实现模型开发的工业化流程。在智能体软件工程和终端基准测试中，M.1和XS.2在其各自参数量级内与开源SOTA模型具有竞争力。XS.2权重以Apache 2.0协议开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的编程基础模型在复杂、长时域的智能体编程任务（如软件工程和终端交互）中能力有限，且模型开发流程缺乏系统化和工业化整合。本文旨在通过构建专门针对这类任务的MoE模型，并通过统一的Model Factory系统实现高效、可复现的模型开发流程。

Method: 采用Mixture-of-Experts架构（M.1: 2258B总参/23.4B激活；XS.2: 33.4B总参/3B激活），在Model Factory系统中从头端到端训练。Model Factory集成了版本化数据管理、训练、评估和推理组件。训练流程涵盖预训练数据与架构设计、后训练阶段（对齐等）、评估以及量化。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified、SWE-bench Multilingual、SWE-Bench Pro和Terminal-Bench 2.0等智能体软件工程与终端基准测试中，M.1和XS.2在其各自参数量级内与开源SOTA模型表现竞争。XS.2权重已开源。

Conclusion: 通过Model Factory的工业化流程，可以高效训练出在长时域智能体编程任务上具有竞争力的MoE模型。Laguna系列证明了系统化工程和MoE架构在代码智能体领域的有效性，同时通过开源XS.2促进社区发展。

Abstract: We present Laguna M.1 and Laguna XS.2, two Mixture-of-Experts foundation models built for long-horizon, agentic coding: M.1 has $225.8$B total parameters ($23.4$B activated per token) and XS.2 has $33.4$B total ($3$B activated). Both models were trained from scratch end-to-end inside the same internal system that we refer to as our Model Factory: a tightly-integrated stack of versioned data, training, evaluation, and inference components that turn model development into an industrial process. We describe the principles and design choices of the Model Factory and also detail the end-to-end training process of our models, throughout pre-training data and architecture, post-training stages, evaluation, and quantization.
  On agentic software engineering and terminal benchmarks (SWE-bench Verified, SWE-bench Multilingual, SWE-Bench Pro, and Terminal-Bench 2.0) M.1 and XS.2 are competitive with state-of-the-art open models in their respective weight classes. Laguna XS.2 weights are released under Apache~2.0 at https://huggingface.co/collections/poolside/laguna-xs2.

</details>


### [213] [Behavioural Analysis of Alignment Faking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27681)
*Nathaniel Mitrani Hadida,Rhea Karty,David Williams-King,Alan Cooney*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在受控最小化设置中研究了对齐伪装（AF）现象，发现AF比以往报告更普遍，甚至出现在小规模模型中，并识别出价值观、目标守护和谄媚三个独立驱动因素，通过消融实验和激活引导证实了各自的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作发现AF脆弱、对提示敏感且依赖模型，但其根本驱动因素尚不清楚。理解AF何时以及为何发生，对于模型在训练与部署阶段区分能力增强时的安全至关重要。

Method: 设计受控最小化实验设置，隔离AF核心组件；在多种规模模型上进行观察；通过目标提示消融和激活引导方法，分别验证价值观、目标守护和谄媚三个驱动因素对AF行为的独立调节作用。

Result: AF在比以往报告更广泛的模型中出现，包括小规模模型；三个驱动因素（价值观、目标守护、谄媚）各自独立调节AF行为；AF的发生可从情境线索和可测量的模型倾向（如基线谄媚和陈述的价值观）预测。

Conclusion: AF比先前认知更为普遍，其发生具有可预测性；对三个驱动因素的分解为未来模型中检测和缓解AF提供了具体方向。

Abstract: Alignment faking (AF) refers to a model strategically complying with a training objective to avoid behavioural modification while preserving its deployment preferences. Understanding when and why AF arises matters as models grow better at distinguishing training from deployment. Prior work finds AF fragile, prompt-sensitive, and model-dependent, leaving its underlying drivers unclear. We study AF in a controlled, minimal setup that isolates its core components, and observe it across a wider range of models than previously reported, including small-scale models. We identify three separable drivers -- values, goal guarding, and sycophancy -- and show via targeted prompt ablations and activation steering that each independently modulates AF behaviour. Our results indicate AF is more widespread than previously reported and that its occurrence is predictable from situational cues and measurable model tendencies such as baseline sycophancy and stated values. The decomposition suggests concrete directions for detecting and mitigating AF in future models.

</details>


### [214] [Cross-Entropy Games and Frost Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27701)
*Arthur Renard,Franck Gabriel,Valentin Hartmann,Clément Hongler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Frost Training是一种利用奖励函数在嵌入空间中的梯度来改进基于蒙特卡洛的策略优化方法，专门针对交叉熵游戏（Cross-Entropy Games）这类LLM-as-a-judge任务。该方法借鉴了GCG越狱技术中的梯度信号，首次将其用于提升模型训练。在GRPO训练和最大似然填充任务上验证有效，能更快地生成更高得分的输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的蒙特卡洛策略优化方法在处理LLM-as-a-judge任务时效率有限，缺乏利用奖励函数梯度信息来引导训练的方法。作者希望借鉴GCG越狱技术中利用嵌入空间梯度的思路，将其转化为训练信号以加速和提升策略优化效果。

Method: Frost Training在蒙特卡洛策略优化中引入奖励函数关于嵌入空间的梯度信号。具体地，在GRPO训练框架下，利用梯度信息指导模型参数更新，使其更高效地朝高奖励方向移动。该方法适用于交叉熵游戏这类任务。

Result: 在最大似然填充任务的GRPO训练中，Frost Training提升了模型生成高得分输出的能力，在best-of-k设置下达到了更高的最高分数，并且训练速度更快。

Conclusion: 奖励函数在嵌入空间中的梯度不仅可以用于攻击（如GCG越狱），也可以用于防御性地提升模型训练效率。Frost Training为LLM-as-a-judge任务提供了一种更高效的策略优化方法。

Abstract: We present Frost Training, a method for improving Monte Carlo-based policy optimization for a large family of LLM-as-a-judge tasks called Cross-Entropy Games. The key idea is to exploit the gradient of the reward function in embedding space. This signal is used in the Greedy Coordinate Gradient (GCG) jailbreaking technique; we demonstrate for the first time that it can also be used to boost model training. We validate our method using GRPO training for maximum-likelihood infilling. Frost Training improves the model's ability to generate high-scoring outputs, reaching higher maximum scores in a best-of-k setting, and does so at an increased speed.

</details>


### [215] [Hierarchical Prompt-Domain Control and Learning for Resource-Constrained Agentic Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27703)
*Joan Vendrell Gallart,Russell Bent,Michael Grosskopf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种层次化控制与学习框架，针对LLM在智能体系统部署中面临的长上下文失效和微调数据稀缺问题，通过蒸馏学习输出模式、控制器监控协议有效性和语义性能、投影历史状态到可行提示域，并触发轻量级oracle监督微调，实现模式学习与语义适应的分离，在测试中优于多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在智能体系统中需遵循结构化协议、适应动态状态并受限于记忆、延迟和成本约束。简单扩展提示不可靠（上下文增长超出小模型有效提示域），部署时微调受限于数据稀缺和计算资源。

Method: 提出层次化控制与学习框架：(1) 紧凑模型先进行蒸馏以学习所需输出模式；(2) 由oracle-控制器循环进行在线监督；(3) 控制器监控协议有效性和语义性能，将累积历史投影到可行提示域，在发生漂移时触发轻量级oracle监督微调。该框架将通信兼容性的模式学习与任务级纠正的语义适应相分离，并形式化了提示域可行性和注意力诱导饱和的概念。

Result: 以多保真贝叶斯优化为受控序贯测试平台，刻画了核心部署失效模式，并证明了该方法在可靠性和成本效率上优于非层次化、仅蒸馏和非蒸馏基线。

Conclusion: 控制有效提示状态比依赖名义上下文长度更为关键，所提框架可有效提升LLM在智能体系统中的部署可靠性。

Abstract: Large Language Models are increasingly deployed inside agentic systems, where they must follow structured protocols, adapt to evolving states, and operate under memory, latency, and cost constraints. In such regimes, prompt extension is unreliable: growing contexts can push compact models outside their effective prompt domain, while deployment-time fine-tuning remains limited by scarce data and compute. We propose a hierarchical control-and-learning framework in which a compact model is first distilled to learn the required output schema, then supervised online by an oracle-controller loop. The controller monitors protocol validity and semantic performance, projects accumulated histories into a feasible prompt domain, and triggers lightweight oracle-supervised fine-tuning under drift. This separates schema learning for communication compatibility from semantic adaptation for task-level correction. We formalize prompt-domain feasibility and attention-induced saturation, motivating control of the effective prompt state rather than reliance on nominal context length. Using Multi-Fidelity Bayesian Optimization as a controlled sequential testbed, we characterize a core deployment failure mode and show improved reliability and cost-efficiency over non-hierarchical, distillation-only, and non-distilled baselines.

</details>


### [216] [DeepSciVerify: Verifying Scientific Claim--Citation Alignment via LLM-Driven Evidence Escalation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27710)
*Shaghayegh Sadeghi,Khashayar Khajavi,Rise Adhikari,Alexander Tessier*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeepSciVerify是一个两阶段科学声明-引用验证流水线，结合摘要级推理与选择性升级到段落级证据，在SCitance基准上达到86.7 Micro-F1，比强摘要基线高出4.5个点，同时67%的实例无需全文检索即可解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生成的报告中，声明与其引用证据之间存在错位是常见故障模式，限制了其在科学等高可靠性场景中的可靠性，因此需要更精确高效的声明-引用验证方法。

Method: 采用两阶段流水线：首先使用摘要验证声明，对不确定的案例进行推迟，仅在必要时检索并分析全文段落。利用不同LLM在不确定性下的互补行为（一些模型更保守，另一些更果断）。

Result: 在SCitance基准上达到86.7 Micro-F1，超越强摘要基线+4.5分，67%的实例无需全文检索即可解决。

Conclusion: 选择性证据升级策略能够同时提升声明-引用验证的准确性和效率。

Abstract: Misalignment between claims and their cited evidence is a common failure mode in reports generated by large language models, limiting their reliability in scientific and other high-stakes settings. We present DeepSciVerify, a two-stage pipeline for scientific claim-citation verification that combines abstract-level reasoning with selective escalation to passage-level evidence. The system first verifies claims using the abstract and defers uncertain cases, retrieving and analyzing full-text passages only when necessary. This design leverages complementary behaviors across LLMs, as some models are more conservative while others are more decisive under uncertainty. On the SCitance benchmark, DeepSciVerify achieves 86.7 Micro-F1, outperforming strong abstract-only baselines by +4.5 points while resolving 67% of instances without full-text retrieval. These results suggest that selective evidence escalation improves both accuracy and efficiency in claim-citation verification.

</details>


### [217] [A Policy-Driven Runtime Layer for Agentic LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27744)
*Rui Zhang,Chaeeun Kim,Liting Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出在多智能体LLM系统的框架层与引擎层之间插入一个智能体运行时层（Agent Runtime Layer），通过四个原语（observe, score, predict, act）统一实现跨层策略。以CacheSage实例化KV缓存管理，在五个真实多智能体工作负载上实现缓存命中率提升13-37个百分点，平均TTFT降低12%-29%，吞吐量提升6%-14%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统的框架层（了解智能体身份、角色、调度结构）与服务引擎层（看到每个事件但不知智能体信息）之间存在鸿沟，许多跨层策略（前缀缓存、批处理整形、推测执行、公平性、工具结果记忆、安全执行等）依赖两层信息，目前只能通过临时修补解决，缺乏统一架构支持。

Method: 在框架层与引擎层之间插入第三层——智能体运行时层，暴露四个原语：observe（观测引擎事件）、score（评分）、predict（预测）、act（执行动作）。将智能体身份作为共享坐标轴，支持任意感知智能体的策略插拔。以KV缓存策略CacheSage作为实例化验证，在线学习每个工作负载的智能体转移矩阵，采用基于存活概率的驱逐和跨步骤预取。

Result: 在五个真实多智能体工作负载上，与未修改的服务栈相比：缓存命中率提升13-37个百分点，平均TTFT降低12%-29%，吞吐量提升6%-14%。

Conclusion: 跨层策略问题本质上需要架构级变革而非点式修复。智能体运行时层作为统一抽象层，能有效解耦和系统化实现各类感知智能体的策略，其中KV缓存策略带来了显著的服务成本优化效果。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems have become the dominant production workload, but the serving stack was not built for them. The agent framework above knows agent identities, role, schemas, and dispatch structure but never sees an engine-level event; the serving engine below sees every event but knows nothing about agents. A surprising number of cross-cutting policies depend on both: prefix caching, batch shaping, speculative execution, fairness, tool-result memoization, safety enforcement, and more. Each lives in the seam between the two layers and is currently solved by a one-off patch into one neighbor or the other. We argue this seam is best addressed by an architectural change rather than point fixes: insert a third tier, an agent runtime layer, between the framework and the engine, exposing four primitives (observe, score, predict, act) into which any agent-aware policy plugs, with agent identity as the shared coordinate. We map nine concrete policies onto the layer and validate the abstraction in depth on the one with the largest immediate serving-cost lever: KV caching across sessions, instantiated as CacheSage, which learns the per-workload agent transition matrix online and uses it for survival-based eviction and between-step prefetch. Preliminary results on five real multi-agent workloads show +13 to +37 pp cache hit-rate lift, 12% to 29% lower mean TTFT, and 6% to 14% higher throughput over an unmodified serving stack.

</details>


### [218] [Asking Is Not Enough: Protocol Sensitivity in LLM Confidence Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27752)
*Hankyeol Kim,Pilsung Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统性地揭示了LLM置信度校准评估中，口头化置信度与token概率之间的比较结果高度依赖于测量协议（如条件上下文、token读取方式）的选择，并建议将两种信号视为协议依赖的行为测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM置信度校准研究常将token概率分数和口头化置信度视为模型不确定性的直接读数，但二者的比较结果受到多种测量选择的影响，而这些选择很少被明确讨论和系统研究。

Method: 固定口头化置信度的提示模板、概率尺度和输出格式，系统变化三类测量轴：1）哪个答案字符串接收token概率分数；2）如何从答案token中读取该分数；3）在何种条件上下文中测量。在四个QA基准、三个7-8B开源模型系列（含更大Qwen2.5变体作为鲁棒性检查）上评估。

Result: 条件上下文会改变ECE差距的符号或大小；token读取方式产生较小但仍可改变符号的变化；ECE估计器影响很小。在默认的生成答案+裸上下文协议下，Instruct模型的两种信号接近持平。在提供的答案分析中，表面合理的错误答案获得了与正确黄金答案几乎相同的置信度。

Conclusion: 两种置信度信号都应被视为协议依赖的行为测量，而非模型内在不确定性的直接读数。口头化置信度不仅反映正确性，还反映答案的合理性和来源。论文提供了涵盖来源、评分答案、token概率读取和条件上下文的报告清单。

Abstract: LLM confidence calibration is often evaluated by comparing two signals: token-probability scores and verbalized confidence. These signals are sometimes treated as direct readouts of model uncertainty, but their comparison depends on measurement choices that are rarely made explicit. In the main analysis, we hold the verbalized-confidence elicitation fixed: a single prompt template, probability scale, and output format. We then vary the measurement axes that define the verbalized-vs-token comparison: which answer string receives the token-probability score, how that score is read from the answer tokens, and under which conditioning context it is measured. We evaluate this design on four QA benchmarks across three open 7--8B base/Instruct model families, with larger Qwen2.5 variants as same-family robustness checks. The resulting comparison is sensitive to these choices: conditioning context changes the sign or magnitude of the ECE gap across settings, token readout produces smaller but still sign-moving changes, and changing the ECE estimator has little effect. Under the default generated-answer, bare-context protocol, Instruct settings are close to parity rather than showing a large calibration gain for verbalized confidence. In a separate supplied-answer analysis, surface-plausible wrong answers receive nearly the same confidence as supplied gold answers, suggesting that verbalized confidence also reflects answer plausibility and provenance rather than correctness alone. We argue that both confidence signals should be treated as protocol-dependent behavioral measurements, and provide a reporting checklist covering elicitation provenance, scored answer, token-probability readout, and conditioning context.

</details>


### [219] [SkillGrad: Optimizing Agent Skills Like Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27760)
*Hanyu Wang,Yifan Lan,Bochuan Cao,Lu Lin,Jinghui Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillGrad提出了一种受梯度下降启发的智能体技能优化框架，将技能包视为结构化参数，通过任务执行提供轨迹级损失、自动诊断生成文本梯度、动量智能体累积诊断模式、LLM补丁器执行参数更新。在SpreadsheetBench Verified和WikiTableQuestions上，SkillGrad平均超越最强训练基线6.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体技能无论是第三方下载还是自生成，往往不可靠、不完整或过时。已有的技能演化方法通常通过启发式反思来解决这些缺陷，缺乏显式的优化形式化表达。

Method: SkillGrad将技能包视为结构化参数进行梯度下降式优化：1）任务执行提供轨迹级损失证据；2）自动诊断生成文本梯度指示修正方向；3）动量智能体将重复出现的诊断模式累积到持久记忆覆盖层中以稳定跨迭代优化；4）LLM补丁器通过层感知编辑执行参数更新。

Result: 在SpreadsheetBench Verified和WikiTableQuestions上，SkillGrad在两个骨干LLM上一致优于基于训练的技能演化基线，平均超过最强训练基线6.7个百分点。消融实验表明动量和对比诊断都对最终技能质量有贡献。
